# SECTION 205-02B: Rear Drive Axle/Differential — Ford 9.75-Inch Ring Gear IN-VEHICLE REPAIR
**2014 F-150 Workshop Manual**
Procedure revision date: 10/25/2013

## Differential Housing Cover

### Material

| Item | Specification |
| :--- | :--- |
| Additive Friction Modifier XL-3 (US); CXL-3 (Canada) | EST-M2C118-A |
| Motorcraft® SAE 75W-140 Synthetic Rear Axle Lubricant XY-75W140-QL (US); CXY-75W140-1L (Canada) | WSL-M2C192-A and GL-5 |
| Silicone Gasket and Sealant TA-30 | WSE-M4G323-A4 |

### Removal and Installation

1. With the vehicle in NEUTRAL, position it on a hoist. For additional information, refer to Section 100-02.
2. Separate the 3 brake tube fasteners from the differential housing cover stud bolts.

![Differential housing cover illustration showing brake tube fasteners](image_placeholder)

3. Remove the differential housing cover.
   1. Remove the 9 differential housing cover bolts and the 3 differential housing cover stud bolts, and drain the lubricant from the axle housing.

Installation
1. NOTE: Remove all of the silicone gasket and make sure the surfaces are free of oil before applying the new silicone gasket.
Clean the gasket mating surface of the axle and the differential housing cover.

2. Apply a new, continuous bead of sealant to the differential housing cover as shown.
N0135100
3. NOTE: The differential housing cover must be installed within 15 minutes of application of the silicone, or new sealant must be applied. If possible, allow one hour before filling with lubricant to make sure the silicone sealant has correctly cured.

4. Remove the fill plug.

5. NOTE: If equipped with Traction-Lok axles, first fill the axle with 118 ml (4 oz) of friction modifier. No friction modifier is used on Electronic Locking Differential (ELD) axles.

Fill the axle with the specified amount of axle lubricant and install the fill plug.
• Tighten to 30 Nm (22 lb-ft).

**Removal**

**All vehicles**

1. Remove the drive pinion flange seal. For additional information, refer to Drive Pinion Flange and Drive Pinion Seal in this section.

2. Remove the differential carrier assembly. For additional information, refer to Differential Carrier in this section.

3. Remove and discard the 12 ring gear bolts.

![DE0309-A]

4. **NOTE:** Do not damage the ring gear bolt hole threads.

   Insert a punch in the ring gear bolt holes and drive the differential ring gear off.

![DE0310-B]

<!-- Page 412 -->
# Electronic Locking Differential (ELD) equipped vehicles

5. Using the Bearing Puller plate, Step Plate and a suitable press, remove the LH differential bearing.

![Diagram showing removal of LH differential bearing using a press](image)
*Labels: 205-D061, 205-D064*

6. Using the 2 Jaw Puller and Step Plate, remove the RH differential bearing.

![Diagram showing removal of RH differential bearing using a 2 Jaw Puller](image)
*Labels: 205-D072, 205-D061*

# Non- ELD equipped vehicles

7. Remove the 2 differential bearings with the 2 Jaw Puller and Step Plate.

![Diagram showing removal of differential bearings using a 2 Jaw Puller](image)
*Labels: 205-D072, 205-D061*

# Installation

## All vehicles

1. Position the Drive Pinion Bearing Cup Installer and the inner and outer drive pinion bearing cups in their respective bores.
   1. Place the Rear Axle Pinion Bearing Cup Installer on the outer drive pinion bearing cup.
   2. Place the Rear Axle Pinion Bearing Cup Installer on the inner drive pinion bearing cup.
   3. Install the Drive Pinion Bearing Cup Installer.

2. Tighten the Drive Pinion Bearing Cup Installer to fully seat the 2 differential drive pinion bearing cups.

3. **NOTE:** If a feeler gauge of the specification shown can be inserted between a cup and the bottom of its bore at any point around the cup, the cup is not correctly seated.

   Make sure the differential pinion bearing cups are correctly seated.

<!-- Page 415 -->
| Item | Part Number | Description |
|:---|:---|:---|
| 1 | 4621 | Outer pinion bearing |
| 2 | 205-160 | Adapter for 205-S127 |
| 3 | 4630 | Inner pinion bearing |
| 4 | 205-383 | Depth Gauge/Aligner, Drive Pinion |
| 5 | 205-159 | Adapter for 205-S127 |
| 6 | 205-377 | Drive Pinion Gauge Tube |
| 7 | 205-937 | Depth Gauge/Aligner, Drive Pinion |
| 8 | 4010 | Rear axle housing outer (front) pinion bearing |

<!-- Page 416 -->
# 9 | 205-111 | Adapter for 205-S127

5. Tighten the Adapter to the specified rotational torque.
   * Tighten to 2.2 Nm (20 lb-in).

![Image 1: Diagram showing tightening of adapter 205-111](image)
*Text in image:* 205-111, ND003075

6. **NOTE:** The Adapter must be offset to obtain an accurate reading.
   Rotate the Adapter several half-turns to make sure of correct seating of the drive pinion bearings.

![Image 2: Diagram showing 45 degree rotation of adapter 205-159](image)
*Text in image:* 205-159, 45°, ND018869

7. Install the Drive Pinion Gauge Tube.
   1. Position the Drive Pinion Gauge Tube.
   2. Install the 2 differential bearing caps.
   3. Install the 4 differential bearing cap bolts.
      * Tighten to 105 Nm (77 lb-ft).

![Image 3: Diagram showing installation of gauge tube 205-377 with callouts 1, 2, 3](image)
*Text in image:* 205-377, 1, 2, 3, ND013855

8. **NOTE:** Drive pinion bearing adjustment shims must be flat and clean.

**NOTE:** A slight drag should be felt for correct drive pinion bearing adjustment shim selection. Do not attempt to force the drive pinion bearing adjustment shim between the gauge block and the gauge tube. This will minimize selection of a drive pinion bearing adjustment shim thicker than required,

# Electronic Locking Differential (ELD) equipped vehicles and all vehicles equipped with 3.55, 3.73, and 4.10 ratio gear sets

16. **NOTE:** The differential flange and ring gear flange must be free of any old retaining compound material. Make sure both surfaces are clean and free of oil, dust and debris. Failure to clean the surfaces can result in ring gear runout concerns.

Clean all traces of the old retaining compound material from the differential flange.
* Use solvent and Scotch-Brite® pads to remove.

17. Apply a one-eighth inch bead of maximum strength retaining compound on the rear face of the ring gear in the pattern shown.

# All vehicles

18. **NOTE:** If removed, press the speed sensor ring on with the differential ring gear.

Install the differential ring gear.
1. Place the differential ring gear on the differential assembly.
2. Hand-start 3 new ring gear bolts to align the holes in the differential ring gear and the differential assembly.
3. Place the differential assembly and differential ring gear onto the press bed blocks with the differential ring gear teeth facing downward.
4. Press the differential ring gear into place.

**Removal**

**NOTICE:** The color on the rear face of the drive pinion nut is critical to this repair. Use the same color new drive pinion nut for installation. If a new collapsible spacer must be installed for pinion bearing preload reduction, install the nut supplied with the new spacer or damage to the component may occur.

1. With the vehicle in NEUTRAL, position it on a hoist. For additional information, refer to Section 100-02.
2. Remove the brake disc. For additional information, refer to Section 206-04.
3. Index-mark the driveshaft flange and pinion flange for correct alignment during installation.

<!-- Page 423 -->
| Step | Image | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 4 | ![Image 1](https://i.imgur.com/placeholder1.png) | Remove the 4 driveshaft flange bolts. |
| 5 | ![Image 2](https://i.imgur.com/placeholder2.png) | **NOTE:** The driveshaft centering socket yoke fits tightly on the pinion flange pilot. Never hammer on the driveshaft or any of its components to disconnect the driveshaft centering socket yoke from the pinion flange. Pry only in the area shown with a suitable tool to disconnect the driveshaft centering socket yoke from the pinion flange.<br><br>Using a suitable tool as shown, disconnect the driveshaft centering socket yoke from the pinion flange.<br>• Using mechanic's wire, position the driveshaft aside. |
| 6 | ![Image 3](https://i.imgur.com/placeholder3.png) | Using a Nm (lb-in) torque wrench on the pinion nut, record the torque required to maintain rotation of the pinion gear through several revolutions. |

# Installation

1. Lubricate the new drive pinion seal with grease.
2. Using the Drive Pinion Oil Seal Installer, install a new drive pinion seal.

![Diagram showing seal installation with label 205-208](image)
*Code: N0015303*

3. Lubricate the pinion flange splines with axle lubricant.
4. **NOTE:** Disregard the scribe marks if a new pinion flange is being installed.
   Align the pinion flange with the drive pinion shaft.

![Diagram showing pinion flange alignment](image)
*Code: DE0411-C*

5. Using the Drive Pinion Flange Installer, install the pinion flange.

**Removal**

1. Remove the differential carrier. For additional information, refer to Differential Carrier in this section.
2. Using the Bearing Puller plate, Step Plate and a suitable press, remove the LH differential bearing.

![Diagram showing parts labeled 205-D061 and 205-D064](image)

3. Remove and discard the 6 screws and 3 stoppers that hold the Electronic Locking Differential (ELD) coil in place and remove the coil.

**Removal**

1. Remove the axle shaft. For additional information, refer to Axle Shaft in this section.
2. **NOTE:** If only a new axle shaft oil seal needs to be installed, use care to avoid damaging the axle shaft oil seal bore.
   Using a suitable seal remover, remove and discard the axle shaft oil seal.
3. Inspect the rear wheel bearing and axle shaft for wear or damage.
4. Using the Axle Bearing Remover and Slide Hammer, remove the rear wheel bearing.

<!-- Page 435 -->
# Installation

1. Lubricate the new rear wheel bearing with axle lubricant.

2. Using the Axle Bearing Installer and Handle, install the rear wheel bearing.

![Diagram showing installation of rear wheel bearing with tools 205-153 and 205-382](image)

3. Lubricate the lip of the new axle shaft oil seal with grease.

4. Using the Axle Oil Seal Installer and Handle, install the new axle shaft oil seal.

![Diagram showing installation of axle shaft oil seal with tools 205-153 and 205-381](image)

5. Install the axle shaft. For additional information, refer to [Axle Shaft](https://www.google.com/search?q=Axle+Shaft) in this section.

SECTION 205-02B: Rear Drive Axle/Differential — Ford 9.75-Inch Ring Gear
REMOVAL AND INSTALLATION
2014 F-150 Workshop Manual
Procedure revision date: 10/25/2013

Axle Assembly

Removal and Installation

All vehicles

NOTICE: Suspension fasteners are critical components because they affect performance of vital components and systems and their failure can result in major service expense. Install new components with the same component number or an equivalent component if installation is necessary. Do not use an installation component of lesser quality or substitute design. Torque values must be used as specified during reassembly to make sure of correct retention of these components.

1. Remove the driveshaft. For additional information, refer to Section 205-01.

<!-- Page 438 -->
```markdown
2. Remove the axle shafts. For additional information, refer to Axle Shaft in this section.

3. Release the parking brake cable tension. For additional information, refer to Section 206-05.

4. Disconnect the parking brake cables.

![ND102575]

5. Disconnect the wheel speed sensor harness retainers from the axle.

**Vehicles with Electronic Locking Differential (ELD)**

6. Disconnect the ELD electrical connector.

**All vehicles**

7. NOTE: If the vent hose is disconnected from the vehicle body, a new retainer must be installed.

Disconnect the vent hose and remove the axle vent.

* To install, tighten to 18 Nm (159 lb-in).

![ND090829]

8. Remove the RH parking brake cable bracket bolt.

* To install, tighten to 30 Nm (22 lb-ft).
```

<!-- Page 439 -->
```markdown
![N0090828](image)

9. Remove the brake tube/parking brake cable bracket bolt.
   • To install, tighten to 25 Nm (18 ft-lb).

![N0102578](image)

10. Disconnect the brake tube from the brake tube retaining clip.

![N0102577](image)

11. Remove the 2 brake hose bracket bolts.
   • To install, tighten to 30 Nm (22 ft-lb).

![N0090825](image)

12. Support the axle with a suitable transmission jack.
```

<!-- Page 440 -->
13. **WARNING:** Do not apply heat or flame to the shock absorber or strut tube. The shock absorber and strut tube are gas pressurized and could explode if heated. Failure to follow this instruction may result in serious personal injury.

Remove and discard the 2 lower shock absorber nuts and 2 bolts.
* To install, tighten the new nuts to 90 Nm (66 lb-ft).

![Diagram showing shock absorber mount area with label N0102576]

14. **NOTICE:** Final tightening of the U-bolt nuts must be done with the suspension at curb height or incorrect clamp load may occur.

Remove and discard the 8 U-bolt nuts and the 4 U-bolts.
* To install, with the suspension at curb height, tighten the new nuts evenly in a cross-type pattern in 4 stages.
    * Stage 1: Tighten to 35 Nm (26 lb-ft).
    * Stage 2: Tighten to 70 Nm (52 lb-ft).
    * Stage 3: Tighten to 100 Nm (74 lb-ft).
    * Stage 4: Tighten to 133 Nm (98 lb-ft).

![Diagram showing U-bolt installation on axle with label N0090824]

15. **WARNING:** Always secure transmission, transfer case, and axle assemblies to their service jack. Avoid obstructions while lowering and raising the jack. Improperly secured assemblies or contact with obstructions may cause the assembly to fall off the jack, which could result in serious personal injury.

Lower and remove the axle assembly.

16. To install the original assembly, reverse the removal procedure.

17. Check the rear axle fluid level after installation.

<!-- Page 451 -->
# SECTION 205-03: Front Drive Axle/Differential
## DESCRIPTION AND OPERATION

**2014 F-150 Workshop Manual**
Procedure revision date: 10/25/2013

---

# Front Drive Axle and Differential

The front drive axle consists of:

*   a cast-aluminum center section, a removable cast-aluminum axle housing tube and a differential housing cover. Both the axle housing tube and the differential housing cover use silicone sealant as a gasket.
*   a hypoid-design gearset of an 8.8-inch ring gear and a pinion gear. Two opposed tapered roller bearings support the drive pinion in the axle housing.
*   a drive pinion collapsible spacer, located on the pinion shaft, maintains pinion bearing preload. The pinion nut adjusts the preload.
*   differential bearing shims, located between the differential bearing cups and the axle housing, adjust the differential bearing preload and the ring gear backlash.
*   a differential case, a 1-piece design with 2 openings to allow for assembly of the internal components and lubricant flow. Two opposed tapered roller bearings (differential bearings) support the differential case in the axle housing. Removable differential bearing caps retain the differential assembly in the axle housing.
*   inside the differential case, the differential pinion shaft supports 2 differential pinion gears. The pinion gear speed is synchronized by a differential clutch spring. The pinion gears engage the differential side gears, to which the left axle shaft and the right axle shaft are splined. The differential pinion shaft bolt retains the differential pinion shaft in the differential case.
*   The bar code tag located on the differential cover contains the axle information.

The front drive axle receives input from the transfer case by way of the front driveshaft. Rotational torque is transferred through the drive pinion to the ring gear, which in turn drives the differential case. Inside the differential case are the pinion gears which rotate on the stationary pinion shaft and the side gears which are splined to the axle shafts. The differential assembly is a gear arrangement that allows the drive wheels to be driven at different speeds and divides the input torque of the pinion gear between the axle halfshafts. The front axle transmits torque only and carries no vehicle load. CV joints located at each end of the halfshafts allow the axle to remain stationary in relationship to front wheels, yet articulate at the knuckle. These CV joints permit the wheels to be driven and turned at the same time. Lack of lubrication is among the most common causes of CV joint concerns. It is essential that all CV boots and clamps be inspected for damage or signs of leakage. Refer to [Section 205-04](#).

The Torsen® front differential case houses the parallel axis planetary helical side gears to which the axle halfshafts are splined. It is the unique arrangement of the differential that allows the continuous, uninterrupted torque output and allows the axle halfshafts to be driven at different speed when needed, such as when driving around corners. There are no clutch plates or discs in this axle design. The Torsen® differential requires 4.0 oz of friction modifier to be added with the axle lubricant. The axle shafts are retained in this differential with the circlips.

```markdown
42. **NOTICE:** Overspreading may damage the differential housing.

**NOTE:** Tighten and loosen the differential carrier spreader adapters to normalize the housing adapters prior to taking the final Dial Indicator reading.

Spread the differential housing to the specification.
1. Adjust the Dial Indicator Gauge to zero.
2. Tighten the screw until spreading the differential housing to the specifications.
3. Remove the Dial Indicator Gauge with Holding Fixture and Clutch Housing Gauge.

![Diagram showing differential housing spread measurement](https://i.imgur.com/placeholder1.png)
*Labels: 100-002, 308-021, 0.762 mm (0.030 in)*

43. **NOTICE:** To avoid galling the case, insert the shims with a light coating of grease before installing the differential carrier.

Place the differential bearing shims in the differential housing.

![Diagram showing shim placement](https://i.imgur.com/placeholder2.png)

44. Install the differential carrier.
1. Position the 2 differential bearing cups on the differential bearings.
2. Lower the differential carrier in place between the differential bearing shims.

![Diagram showing differential carrier installation](https://i.imgur.com/placeholder3.png)

45. **NOTE:** Hand-tighten the differential bearing cap bolts prior to releasing the Differential Carrier Spreader.
```

<!-- Page 485 -->
```markdown
| Image | Text |
|-------|------|
| ![Image 1](ND009898) | Install the 2 differential bearing caps and 4 bolts in their original location. |
| ![Image 2](ND014143) | 46. Loosen and remove the Differential Carrier Spreader. |
| ![Image 3](ND014144) | 47. Install the Dial Indicator Gauge with Holding Fixture at the 12 o’clock position. |
| ![Image 4](ND013688) | 48. Using the Dial Indicator Gauge with Holding Fixture and Clutch Housing Gauge, recheck the ring gear backlash. |
| | 49. Apply marking compound and rotate the differential assembly 5 complete revolutions. |
```

<!-- Page 486 -->
50. Verify an acceptable pattern check. For additional information, refer to Diagnosis and Testing in this section.

51. Assemble the axle housing tube.
* Align the bolt holes and position the axle tube.
* Position the axle identification tag.
* Install the 4 bolts.
    * Tighten to 73 Nm (54 lb-ft).

52. Install a new stub shaft seal. For additional information, refer to Stub Shaft Pilot Bearing and Seal in this section.

53. Install a new circlip on the intermediate shaft end.

54. Insert the intermediate shaft and engage the circlip with the side gears.

55. Remove the axle assembly from the Holding Fixture and place it on the bench.

56. NOTE: Remove all of the silicone gasket and make sure the surfaces are free of oil before applying the new silicone gasket.

NOTE: The differential housing cover must be installed within 15 minutes of application of the silicone, or new sealant must be applied. If possible, allow one hour before filling with lubricant to make sure the silicone sealant has correctly cured.

Apply a continuous bead of sealant, of the specified thickness, to the differential housing cover mounting surface as shown in the illustration.
* Use silicone gasket and sealant.

<!-- Page 487 -->
```markdown
| Image | Description |
|-------|-------------|
| ![Diagram of differential housing cover](A0005416) | 3.18-4.76 mm (1/8-3/16 in.) |
| ![Diagram of axle assembly](A0083417) | |

57. Install the differential housing cover and the 10 cover bolts.
   * Tighten to 32 Nm (24 ft-lb).

58. Install the axle assembly. For additional information, refer to [Axle Assembly](#) in this section.

59. Remove the fill plug and fill the axle assembly with the specified type and amount of lubricant.
   * Tighten the fill plug to 25 Nm (18 lb-ft).
```

**Removal**

_NOTICE:_ The color on the rear face of the drive pinion nut is critical to this repair. Use the same color new drive pinion nut for installation as the original. If a new collapsible spacer must be installed for pinion bearing preload reduction, install the nut supplied with the new spacer or damage to the component may occur.

_NOTICE:_ This operation disturbs the pinion bearing preload. Carefully reset the pinion bearing preload during assembly.

1. With the vehicle in NEUTRAL, position it on a hoist. Refer to Section 100-02.
2. Remove the front brake pads. Refer to Section 206-03.
3. Remove the front driveshaft. Refer to Section 205-01.
4. Measure and record the pinion bearing preload.
   * Using a Nm (lb-in) torque wrench, rotate the pinion gear. Measure the torque required to maintain pinion gear rotation.

<!-- Page 496 -->
```markdown
<predict_bbox>[[178, 140, 460, 273], [178, 313, 460, 447], [178, 553, 460, 687], [178, 733, 460, 867]]</predict_bbox>
<predict_classes>["image", "image", "image", "image"]</predict_classes>
<output_markdown>
![DE2719-A](image)
5. Index-mark the pinion flange and the drive pinion gear.

![DE2714-A](image)
6. **NOTICE:** Install a new pinion nut with the same color as the original if not replacing the collapsible spacer. If a new collapsible spacer is installed, install the nut in the kit or damage to the component may occur.

Using the Drive Pinion Flange Holding Fixture to hold the pinion flange, remove and discard the pinion nut and washer.

![ND015191](image)
205-126

7. Using the 2 Jaw Puller, remove the pinion flange.

![ND015168](image)
205-D072

```

<!-- Page 497 -->
```markdown
8. Inspect the pinion flange for burrs and damage. Inspect the end of the pinion flange that contacts the pinion bearing cone, pinion nut counterbore and drive pinion seal surface for nicks. Discard the pinion flange if damaged.

9. **NOTICE:** Do not damage the axle housing.

Force up on the metal flange of the drive pinion seal. Install gripping pliers and strike with a hammer to remove the drive pinion seal.

![A0008803](image)

10. Verify the splines on the drive pinion gear are free of burrs. If burrs are evident, remove them with a fine crocus cloth.

11. Clean the drive pinion seal bore.

## Installation

1. **NOTE:** Lubricate the drive pinion seal lips with axle lubricant.

**NOTE:** Drive pinion seal must be fully seated all the way around or drive pinion seal damage will occur.

Using the Drive Pinion Oil Seal Installer, install the drive pinion seal.

![N0113709 205-208](image)

2. **NOTICE:** Never use a hammer or install the pinion flange with power tools.

**NOTE:** Lubricate the pinion flange splines with axle lubricant.

Align the index marks made during removal and, using the Drive Pinion Flange Installer, install the pinion flange.
```

**Removal and Installation**

1. Remove the front driveshaft. Refer to Section 205-01.
2. Remove the halfshafts. Refer to Section 205-04.
3. Remove the 4 crossmember nuts and bolts and the crossmember.

![Diagram GE0586-B]

   * To install, tighten to 175 Nm (129 lb-ft).

![Diagram N0054209]

6. Remove the front axle housing bushing bolt.
   * To install, tighten to 175 Nm (129 lb-ft).

# Technical Manual Page Content

## Visual Elements

### Diagram 1: Pinion Flange Positioning
*   **Label:** DE0496-A

## Text Instructions

**18.** Position the pinion flange.

**19.** Using the Drive Pinion Flange Installer, install the pinion flange.

**20.** Apply a small amount of axle lubricant to the washer side of the new pinion nut and install the new pinion nut.

**21.** **NOTICE:** Do not loosen the pinion nut to reduce drive pinion bearing preload under any circumstance. If it is necessary to reduce drive pinion bearing preload, install a new drive pinion collapsible spacer and pinion nut or damage to the component may occur.

Install the Drive Pinion Flange Holding Fixture and tighten the pinion nut. Refer to the torque specifications in the Specifications portion of this section.
* Rotate the drive pinion gear occasionally to make sure the drive pinion bearings are seating correctly. Take frequent drive pinion bearing preload readings by rotating the drive pinion gear with a Nm (lb-in) torque wrench.

## Step 22

22. Press the left and right differential bearing on the differential case.
1. Position the differential bearing.
2. Using the Differential Side Bearing Installer, press the differential bearing on the differential case.
   • Repeat for the other side.

## Step 23
**Image Label:** N0015185
**Tool Label:** 205-010

**Text:**
23. Install the differential carrier.
1. Position the 2 differential bearing cups on the differential bearings.
2. Lower the differential carrier in place.

## Step 24
**Image Label:** N0014142

**Text:**
24. Install a differential bearing shim of the thickness shown on the LH side of the differential bearing.

<!-- Page 531 -->

25. **NOTE:** Apply pressure toward the left side to make sure the left bearing cap is seated.

Install the left bearing cap and loosely install the 2 bearing cap bolts.

26. **NOTE:** Apply pressure toward the left side to make sure the left bearing cap is seated.

Install progressively thicker differential bearing shims on the right side until the thickest differential bearing shim can be inserted by hand.

27. Install the right bearing cap and 2 bolts.

28. Tighten the 4 differential bearing cap bolts.
* Tighten to 105 Nm (77 lb-ft).

29. Rotate the differential carrier to make sure it turns freely.

30. Install the Dial Indicator Gauge with Holding Fixture at the 12 o’clock position.

## Text Block 31
31. Using the Dial Indicator Gauge with Holding Fixture and Clutch Housing Gauge, measure the differential ring gear backlash at 4 equally spaced points.
1. Attach the Dial Indicator Gauge with Holding Fixture and Clutch Housing Gauge.
2. Position the Dial Indicator Gauge with Holding Fixture and Clutch Housing Gauge tip centrally on a drive tooth.
3. Zero the Dial Indicator.
4. Turn the differential ring gear without turning the drive pinion gear. Record the indicator reading. The allowable backlash is 0.203 mm (0.008 in) to 0.305 mm (0.012 in) and must not vary more than 0.101 mm (0.004 in) between points measured.
To correct for a high or low backlash, proceed as follows.

## Diagram 2
![Diagram 2](image)
Labels: 1, 2, 3, 4, 100-002, 308-021
Code: ND014145

## Text Block 32
32. To correct for high or low backlash, increase the thickness of one differential bearing shim and decrease the thickness of the other differential bearing shim by the same amount. Refer to the following tables when adjusting the backlash.

## Table
| Backlash Change Required | Thickness Change Required |
| :--- | :--- |
| mm | Inch | mm | Inch |
| 0.025 | 0.001 | 0.050 | 0.002 |
| 0.050 | 0.002 | 0.050 | 0.002 |
| 0.076 | 0.003 | 0.101 | 0.004 |

33. Remove the 4 differential bearing cap bolts.
• Remove the differential carrier.

34. Measure the thickness of the selected right and left bearing shims.

35. To establish differential bearing preload, increase both the left and right differential bearing shim thickness by 0.203 mm (0.008 in).

36. Install the Dial Indicator Gauge with Holding Fixture, Clutch Housing Gauge and Differential Carrier Spreader.

![Technical drawing with labels 100-002, 308-021, 205-001](image)

37. **NOTICE:** Overspreading may damage the differential housing.

**NOTE:** Tighten and loosen the differential carrier spreader screw to normalize the housing spreader adapters prior to taking the final Dial Indicator reading.

Spread the differential housing to the specification.
1. Adjust the Dial Indicator Gauge to zero.
2. Tighten the screw until spreading the differential housing to the specification.
3. Remove the Dial Indicator Gauge with Holding Fixture and Clutch Housing Gauge.

51. Insert the intermediate shaft and engage the circlip with the differential side gear.

* Tighten the fill plug to 25 Nm (18 lb-ft).

# Installation

1. **NOTE:** Make sure the Dial Indicator needle is in the spreader adapter hole.

   Install the Dial Indicator Gauge with Holding Fixture, Clutch Housing Gauge and Differential Carrier Spreader.

2. **NOTICE:** Overspreading may damage the component.

   Spread the axle housing to the specification.
   * Remove the Dial Indicator.

3. **NOTICE:** Never attempt to drive the differential bearing shim in place. Permanent damage to the machined axle housing surfaces may occur.

   Position the differential bearing shims.
   1. Apply a light coating of grease to one side of the differential bearing shims to help hold them in place.
   2. Position the differential bearing shims.

## Reusable Cleaning Script

Save this as `scripts/clean_md.py`. It implements the cleaning rules and can be imported by both the backfill script and the remaining-page extractor.

```python
# scripts/clean_md.py
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

# Common service-manual noise patterns
FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"repair4less", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d+\s*$"),  # lone page numbers at bottom
]

FIGURE_LABEL_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\b(N\d{6}[-A-Z]?|\d{3}-\d{3})\b")

def clean_text(raw: str, page_num: Optional[int] = None) -> str:
    """Apply consistent cleaning rules to extracted manual text."""
    text = raw

    # 1. Remove footer watermarks / noise lines
    lines = text.splitlines()
    cleaned_lines = []
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if any(p.search(stripped) for p in FOOTER_PATTERNS):
            continue
        cleaned_lines.append(line)
    text = "\n".join(cleaned_lines)

    # 2. Fix hyphenation across line breaks (e.g., "lubri-\ncant" -> "lubricant")
    text = re.sub(r"(\w)-\n(\w)", r"\1\2", text)

    # 3. Collapse excessive blank lines (keep paragraph breaks)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)

    # 4. Normalize whitespace within lines but preserve intentional indentation
    lines = [re.sub(r"[ \t]+", " ", line) for line in text.splitlines()]
    text = "\n".join(lines).strip()

    # 5. Inject explicit page marker if provided
    if page_num is not None:
        text = f"---\nPage: {page_num}\n---\n\n{text}"

    return text.strip() + "\n"


def clean_markdown_file(path: Path, page_num: Optional[int] = None) -> str:
    """Read a markdown file, clean it, and return the cleaned string."""
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    return clean_text(raw, page_num=page_num)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python clean_md.py <file.md> [page_num]")
        sys.exit(1)
    p = Path(sys.argv[1])
    page = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else None
    print(clean_markdown_file(p, page_num=page))
```

### What it fixes
- Strips `repair4less` and lone numeric footer lines.
- Joins hyphenated words split across lines.
- Normalizes whitespace and blank lines.
- Adds a front-matter-style `--- Page: N ---` block so every chunk knows its source page.

---

## Step 2 — Extract Remaining Pages with PyMuPDF

Save this as `scripts/extract_remaining.py`. It is **resumable**: it skips any page that already has a corresponding `./md/page_NNN.md` file.

```python
# scripts/extract_remaining.py
import fitz  # PyMuPDF
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from clean_md import clean_text  # adjust import if you package it differently

PDF_PATH = Path("service_manual.pdf")   # <-- change to your actual PDF path
MD_DIR = Path("./md")
START_PAGE = 1   # 1-indexed; adjust to Y+1
END_PAGE = None  # None = end of document

def page_filename(page_num: int) -> str:
    return f"page_{page_num:03d}.md"

def extract_remaining(pdf_path: Path, md_dir: Path, start: int, end: int | None):
    md_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    doc = fitz.open(str(pdf_path))
    total = doc.page_count
    if end is None or end > total:
        end = total

    skipped, extracted = 0, 0
    for idx in range(start - 1, end):  # convert to 0-indexed
        page_num = idx + 1
        out_file = md_dir / page_filename(page_num)
        if out_file.exists():
            skipped += 1
            continue

        page = doc[idx]
        raw_text = page.get_text("text")  # or "blocks" for better layout control
        cleaned = clean_text(raw_text, page_num=page_num)
        out_file.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
        extracted += 1
        print(f"[OK] Page {page_num} -> {out_file.name}")

    doc.close()
    print(f"\nDone. Extracted: {extracted}, Skipped (already exist): {skipped}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Allow CLI overrides: python extract_remaining.py <pdf> <md_dir> [start] [end]
    pdf = Path(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else PDF_PATH
    md = Path(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else MD_DIR
    s = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else START_PAGE
    e = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else END_PAGE
    extract_remaining(pdf, md, s, e)
```

### How to use
1. Place your PDF at the path above (or pass it as the first argument).
2. Run:
   ```bash
   python scripts/extract_remaining.py service_manual.pdf ./md 1 200
   ```
   It will skip pages that already have `./md/page_001.md`, etc., and only extract the missing ones.

### `inspect_and_clean_md.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Step 1: Inspect and clean extracted markdown files from a vehicle service manual.

Usage:
    python inspect_and_clean_md.py --md-dir ./md --report-only
    python inspect_and_clean_md.py --md-dir ./md --clean --output-dir ./md_clean

The script:
1. Reports naming, format, page/heading preservation, and common cleaning problems.
2. Applies deterministic cleaning rules (headers/footers, hyphenation, table repair, whitespace).
3. Writes cleaned files (optional) and a JSON/text report.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Common boilerplate found in service-manual PDFs (tune these after seeing the report)
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^\s*repair4less\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*Page\s+\d+\s+of\s+\d+\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$"),  # standalone page number
]

# A line that looks like a markdown heading
HEADING_RE = re.compile(r"^\s{0,3}#{1,6}\s+.+")

# A line that is part of a markdown table
TABLE_LINE_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*\|")

# Hyphenation across a line break: word-\nword
HYPHEN_BREAK_RE = re.compile(r"(\w)-\n(\w)")

# Multiple blank lines
MULTI_BLANK_RE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")

# Trailing whitespace on lines
TRAILING_WS_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)


def detect_page_number(text: str) -> int | None:
    """Try to find a page number from common markdown artifacts."""
    # Look for patterns like "## Page 34", "Page 34", or a lone number at start/end
    patterns = [
        re.compile(r"#+\s*Page\s*(\d+)", re.IGNORECASE),
        re.compile(r"^Page\s*(\d+)$", re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE),
        re.compile(r"^\s*(\d{1,4})\s*$", re.MULTILINE),
    ]
    for pat in patterns:
        m = pat.search(text)
        if m:
            return int(m.group(1))
    return None


def inspect_file(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    lines = text.splitlines()

    report: dict[str, Any] = {
        "file": path.name,
        "size_bytes": path.stat().st_size,
        "line_count": len(lines),
        "char_count": len(text),
        "detected_page": detect_page_number(text),
        "headings": [],
        "table_lines": 0,
        "list_items": 0,
        "issues": [],
        "first_lines": lines[:5],
        "last_lines": lines[-5:],
    }

    # Headings
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        if HEADING_RE.match(line):
            report["headings"].append({"line": i + 1, "text": line.strip()})

    # Table lines
    report["table_lines"] = sum(1 for l in lines if TABLE_LINE_RE.match(l))

    # List items
    report["list_items"] = sum(1 for l in lines if re.match(r"^\s*[\d\*\-]+\s+[\.\)]?\s", l))

    # Issue detection
    if re.search(r"repair4less", text, re.IGNORECASE):
        report["issues"].append("watermark: repair4less")
    if HYPHEN_BREAK_RE.search(text):
        report["issues"].append("possible hyphenation breaks")
    if MULTI_BLANK_RE.search(text):
        report["issues"].append("multiple consecutive blank lines")
    if TRAILING_WS_RE.search(text):
        report["issues"].append("trailing whitespace")
    # Standalone page numbers
    for i, l in enumerate(lines):
        if re.match(r"^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$", l) and not HEADING_RE.match(l):
            report["issues"].append(f"standalone number on line {i+1}: {l.strip()}")
            break  # report once per file

    return report


def clean_text(text: str, page_num: int | None = None) -> str:
    """Apply deterministic cleaning rules while preserving headings and tables."""
    lines = text.splitlines()
    cleaned_lines: list[str] = []

    for line in lines:
        # 1. Strip trailing whitespace
        line = TRAILING_WS_RE.sub("", line)

        # 2. Drop header/footer boilerplate (but keep headings and table lines)
        if HEADING_RE.match(line) or TABLE_LINE_RE.match(line):
            cleaned_lines.append(line)
            continue

        if any(pat.match(line) for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS):
            continue

        cleaned_lines.append(line)

    text = "\n".join(cleaned_lines)

    # 3. Fix hyphenation breaks across newlines (only outside tables)
    # We do this line-by-line to avoid mangling table pipes.
    out_lines: list[str] = []
    prev_line = ""
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if TABLE_LINE_RE.match(line) or HEADING_RE.match(line):
            out_lines.append(line)
            prev_line = line
            continue
        if prev_line and HYPHEN_BREAK_RE.search(prev_line + "\n" + line):
            # Join "word-\nword" -> "wordword" (conservative: only if both sides are word chars)
            merged = HYPHEN_BREAK_RE.sub(r"\1\2", prev_line + "\n" + line)
            # The regex above operates on the combined string; we need to split back.
            # Simpler approach: just remove the hyphen at end of prev_line if next starts with word char.
            if re.search(r"-\s*$", prev_line) and re.match(r"\w", line):
                prev_line = prev_line.rstrip("-").rstrip()
                # Do not add a space; hyphenated word split across lines
                out_lines[-1] = prev_line
                line = line  # current line will be added below
            else:
                out_lines.append(line)
        else:
            out_lines.append(line)
        prev_line = line

    text = "\n".join(out_lines)

    # 4. Collapse 3+ blank lines to 2
    text = MULTI_BLANK_RE.sub("\n\n", text)

    # 5. Ensure file ends with single newline
    text = text.strip() + "\n"

    return text


def repair_tables_in_text(text: str) -> str:
    """
    Very light table repair: ensure every row in a markdown table block has the
    same number of pipe-separated cells by padding with empty cells.
    """
    lines = text.splitlines()
    out: list[str] = []
    in_table = False
    max_cells = 0
    table_block: list[str] = []

    def flush_table() -> None:
        nonlocal table_block, max_cells, in_table
        if not table_block:
            return
        # Pad rows
        padded = []
        for row in table_block:
            cells = row.split("|")
            # Remove empty leading/trailing from split if row starts/ends with |
            if row.startswith("|"):
                cells = cells[1:]
            if row.endswith("|"):
                cells = cells[:-1]
            while len(cells) < max_cells:
                cells.append("")
            padded.append("| " + " | ".join(cells) + " |")
        out.extend(padded)
        table_block = []
        max_cells = 0
        in_table = False

    for line in lines:
        if TABLE_LINE_RE.match(line):
            if not in_table:
                in_table = True
                table_block = []
            cells = line.split("|")
            if line.startswith("|"):
                cells = cells[1:]
            if line.endswith("|"):
                cells = cells[:-1]
            max_cells = max(max_cells, len(cells))
            table_block.append(line)
        else:
            flush_table()
            out.append(line)
    flush_table()

    return "\n".join(out)


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Inspect and clean extracted markdown.")
    parser.add_argument("--md-dir", default="./md", help="Directory containing extracted .md files")
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", default=None, help="If set, write cleaned files here")
    parser.add_argument("--clean", action="store_true", help="Actually clean files (default: report only)")
    parser.add_argument("--report", default="cleaning_report.json", help="Report output path")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    md_dir = Path(args.md_dir)
    if not md_dir.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {md_dir} does not exist.", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

    files = sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {md_dir}\n")

    full_report: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    for path in files:
        rep = inspect_file(path)
        full_report.append(rep)

        print(f"--- {path.name} ---")
        print(f"  Size: {rep['size_bytes']} bytes | Lines: {rep['line_count']} | Chars: {rep['char_count']}")
        print(f"  Detected page: {rep['detected_page']}")
        print(f"  Headings: {len(rep['headings'])} | Table lines: {rep['table_lines']} | List items: {rep['list_items']}")
        if rep["issues"]:
            print(f"  Issues: {', '.join(rep['issues'])}")
        else:
            print("  Issues: none detected")
        print(f"  First lines: {rep['first_lines']}")
        print(f"  Last lines:  {rep['last_lines']}")
        print()

    # Write report
    report_path = Path(args.report)
    report_path.write_text(json.dumps(full_report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Report written to {report_path}")

    # Optional cleaning
    if args.clean:
        out_dir = Path(args.output_dir) if args.output_dir else md_dir
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        for path in files:
            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
            cleaned = clean_text(text, detect_page_number(text))
            cleaned = repair_tables_in_text(cleaned)
            out_path = out_dir / path.name
            out_path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
            print(f"Cleaned: {out_path}")

        print(f"\nCleaned files written to {out_dir}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## Step 1 — Inspection & Cleaning Scripts

### 1.1 Inspection script (`inspect_md.py`)

Save this as `inspect_md.py` in your project root and run `python inspect_md.py`.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Inspect existing ./md/*.md files.
Reports: naming, page/heading preservation, and common cleaning problems.
"""
import re
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

PAGE_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^#+\s*Page\s+(\d+)", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"Page\s+(\d+)\s*(?:of\s+(\d+))?", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*(\d{1,3})\s*$"),  # lone page number
]


def inspect():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR.resolve()} does not exist.")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Markdown files found: {len(files)} in {MD_DIR.resolve()}\n")
    print("=" * 90)

    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        lines = text.splitlines()

        print(f"\nFILE: {f.name}")
        print(f"  Size: {f.stat().st_size:,} bytes | Lines: {len(lines)} | Chars: {len(text):,}")

        # --- Page number detection ---
        page_markers = []
        for pat in PAGE_PATTERNS:
            for i, line in enumerate(lines):
                m = pat.search(line)
                if m:
                    page_markers.append((i + 1, m.group(1), line.strip()[:70]))
                    break
            if page_markers:
                break

        if page_markers:
            print(f"  Page marker: line {page_markers[0][0]} -> '{page_markers[0][1]}' "
                  f"(context: {page_markers[0][2]})")
        else:
            print("  [ISSUE] No clear page-number marker found")

        # --- Headings ---
        headings = [l for l in lines if l.strip().startswith("#")]
        print(f"  Markdown headings: {len(headings)}")
        if headings:
            print(f"  Sample headings: {headings[:3]}")

        # --- Cleaning problems ---
        issues = []

        # Repeated short lines (headers/footers)
        line_counter = Counter(l.strip() for l in lines if l.strip())
        repeated = [l for l, c in line_counter.items() if c > 3 and len(l) < 100]
        if repeated:
            issues.append(f"Repeated short lines (possible header/footer): {repeated[:5]}")

        # Inconsistent table columns
        table_lines = [l for l in lines if "|" in l]
        if table_lines:
            pipe_counts = {l.count("|") for l in table_lines}
            if len(pipe_counts) > 1:
                issues.append(f"Inconsistent table pipe counts: {sorted(pipe_counts)}")

        # Hyphenation across line breaks
        hyphen_breaks = re.findall(r"(\w+)-\n(\w+)", text)
        if hyphen_breaks:
            issues.append(f"Possible hyphenation splits: {hyphen_breaks[:5]}")

        # Control characters
        control_chars = re.findall(r"[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f]", text)
        if control_chars:
            issues.append(f"Control characters found: {len(control_chars)}")

        # Very long lines (possible bad table flattening)
        long_lines = [l for l in lines if len(l) > 500]
        if long_lines:
            issues.append(f"Lines >500 chars: {len(long_lines)} (possible flattened table)")

        if issues:
            print("  ISSUES:")
            for iss in issues:
                print(f"    - {iss}")
        else:
            print("  No obvious cleaning issues detected")

        print("-" * 90)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    inspect()
```

### What the source material looks like (from your image)

| Feature | Observation on pages 44–50 |
|---------|---------------------------|
| **Numbered procedure steps** | `44.`, `45.`, `46.` … `50.` |
| **Torque specs** | `105 Nm (77 lb-ft)`, `73 Nm (54 ft-lb)` |
| **Figure callouts** | `100-002`, `ND014144`, `ND034383` embedded near illustrations |
| **Page numbers** | Visible as standalone numbers (44–50) |
| **Watermark** | `repair4less` at the bottom of the page |
| **Section headings** | Not visible in this snippet, but likely present on other pages |
| **Tables** | None on this page, but service manuals usually contain spec tables elsewhere |

### Anticipated / observed cleaning problems
1. **Headers/footers** – standalone page numbers and the `repair4less` watermark will pollute chunks.
2. **Figure artifacts** – codes like `100-002` and `ND034383` appear mid-text and should be treated as figure references, not body copy.
3. **Hyphenation / line breaks** – PDF extraction often splits words across lines.
4. **Mixed units** – `Nm` and `lb-ft` appear together; the pipeline must preserve both or normalize them later.
5. **Page metadata** – page numbers are likely in filenames or as standalone lines; they need to be captured in frontmatter for citation.

### Reusable cleaning script

Save this as `scripts/clean_md.py`. It reads `./md/*.md`, applies the same rules to every file, and writes cleaned Markdown (with YAML frontmatter) to `./md_clean/`. You can then replace `./md/` with the cleaned output.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Reusable Markdown cleaner for service-manual PDF extraction.

Rules:
- Removes standalone page-number lines
- Removes 'repair4less' watermark lines
- Collapses 3+ blank lines to 2
- Strips trailing whitespace
- Adds YAML frontmatter with page number (parsed from filename)
- Preserves figure codes, torque specs, and numbered steps
"""

from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys

INPUT_DIR = Path("./md")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("./md_clean")

# --- Cleaning patterns ---
STANDALONE_PAGE_NUM = re.compile(r"^\s*\d{1,3}\s*$")
WATERMARK = re.compile(r"repair4less", re.IGNORECASE)
MULTI_BLANK = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")
TRAILING_WS = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)


def extract_page_from_filename(name: str) -> int | None:
    """Pull the first integer from a filename like page_044.md."""
    m = re.search(r"(\d+)", Path(name).stem)
    return int(m.group(1)) if m else None


def clean_text(raw: str, page_num: int | None = None) -> str:
    lines = raw.splitlines()
    kept: list[str] = []

    for line in lines:
        line = TRAILING_WS.sub("", line)

        # Drop standalone page numbers (e.g., "44" on its own line)
        if STANDALONE_PAGE_NUM.match(line):
            continue

        # Drop watermark-only lines
        if WATERMARK.search(line) and len(line.strip()) < 40:
            continue

        kept.append(line)

    text = "\n".join(kept)
    text = MULTI_BLANK.sub("\n\n", text)
    text = text.strip()
    return text


def main() -> None:
    if not INPUT_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {INPUT_DIR} not found. Create it or adjust INPUT_DIR.", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    md_files = sorted(INPUT_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    if not md_files:
        print(f"No markdown files found in {INPUT_DIR}")
        return

    for md_file in md_files:
        page = extract_page_from_filename(md_file.name)
        raw = md_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        cleaned = clean_text(raw, page)

        frontmatter = ""
        if page is not None:
            frontmatter = f"---\npage: {page}\nsource: service_manual.pdf\n---\n\n"

        out_path = OUTPUT_DIR / md_file.name
        out_path.write_text(frontmatter + cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"cleaned {md_file.name:20s} -> {out_path}  (page={page})")

    print(f"\nDone. {len(md_files)} file(s) written to {OUTPUT_DIR}/")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### `inspect_md.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Inspect extracted markdown files in ./md/ for:
- naming convention
- page number and heading preservation
- common cleaning problems (headers/footers, broken tables, hyphenation)

Outputs a JSON report to stdout.
"""
import argparse
import json
import re
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path


def extract_page_number(text: str, filename: Path):
    """Try frontmatter, markdown heading, then filename."""
    # YAML frontmatter: page: 51
    m = re.search(r"---\s*\n.*?page:\s*(\d+)", text, re.S | re.I)
    if m:
        return int(m.group(1))
    # H1/H2 like "# Page 51" or "## 51. ..."
    m = re.search(r"^#{1,6}\s*(?:Page\s*)?(\d+)", text, re.M | re.I)
    if m:
        return int(m.group(1))
    # Fallback: digits in filename
    m = re.search(r"(\d+)", filename.stem)
    if m:
        return int(m.group(1))
    return None


def inspect(md_dir: Path):
    files = sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))
    if not files:
        print(json.dumps({"error": f"No .md files found in {md_dir}"}, indent=2))
        return

    report = {
        "md_dir": str(md_dir),
        "total_files": len(files),
        "files": [],
        "summary": {},
    }

    line_counter: Counter[str] = Counter()
    all_headings = []

    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        lines = text.splitlines()

        for ln in lines:
            line_counter[ln.strip()] += 1

        page = extract_page_number(text, f)
        headings = re.findall(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)", text, re.M)
        all_headings.extend([(f.name, h[0], h[1]) for h in headings])

        # Heuristic issue detection
        issues = []

        # Hyphenation at line break (word split across pages/lines)
        hyphenated = [ln for ln in lines if ln.rstrip().endswith("-") and len(ln.strip()) > 1]
        if hyphenated:
            issues.append(f"possible_hyphenation: {len(hyphenated)} lines end with '-'")

        # Suspiciously short pages (likely extraction failure or image-only page)
        if len(text.strip()) < 100:
            issues.append("very_short_content: likely extraction failure or image-only page")

        # Non-ASCII / replacement characters
        if "\ufffd" in text:
            issues.append("unicode_replacement_char: contains U+FFFD")

        # Multiple spaces / weird whitespace
        if re.search(r"[ \t]{3,}", text):
            issues.append("excessive_whitespace: runs of 3+ spaces/tabs")

        report["files"].append({
            "file": f.name,
            "page": page,
            "chars": len(text),
            "lines": len(lines),
            "headings": headings,
            "issues": issues,
            "preview": text[:160].replace("\n", " | "),
        })

    # Repeated lines across >50% of files = likely running header/footer
    total = len(files)
    repeated = {
        line: count
        for line, count in line_counter.items()
        if count > total * 0.5 and len(line.strip()) > 0
    }
    report["repeated_lines_possible_headers_footers"] = dict(
        sorted(repeated.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])
    )

    # Broken table heuristic: lines with pipes that don't look like a clean row
    broken_tables = []
    for f in files:
        for i, ln in enumerate(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(), start=1):
            if "|" in ln:
                pipes = ln.count("|")
                # A markdown table row usually has >= 2 pipes; 1 pipe or odd patterns may be broken
                if pipes == 1 or (pipes > 0 and pipes % 2 == 0 and "---" not in ln):
                    broken_tables.append({
                        "file": f.name,
                        "line": i,
                        "content": ln[:120],
                    })
    report["possible_broken_tables"] = broken_tables[:100]

    # Summary stats
    report["summary"] = {
        "files_with_issues": sum(1 for x in report["files"] if x["issues"]),
        "files_missing_page_number": sum(1 for x in report["files"] if x["page"] is None),
        "total_headings_found": len(all_headings),
        "sample_headings": all_headings[:10],
    }

    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Inspect ./md/ extraction quality")
    parser.add_argument("--md_dir", default="./md", help="Directory containing extracted markdown files")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    inspect(Path(args.md_dir))
```

### `inspect_md.py` (run this first)

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Inspect existing ./md/ files for naming, page/heading preservation,
and common PDF-extraction cleaning problems.
"""
import os
import re
import json
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

MD_DIR = Path("./md")
REPORT_PATH = Path("inspection_report.json")

# --- Patterns ---
PAGE_NUM_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^Page\s+\d+$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\d{1,4}$"),                          # bare number
    re.compile(r"^-\s*\d+\s*-$"),                      # - 42 -
    re.compile(r"^©.*\d{4}"),                          # copyright line
]
HEADING_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^#{1,6}\s+"),                         # markdown # heading
    re.compile(r"^\d+(\.\d+)*\s+[A-Z]"),               # 4.2 Section Title
    re.compile(r"^[A-Z][A-Z\s]{3,}$"),                 # ALL CAPS heading
]
HYPHENATION_PATTERN = re.compile(r"-\s*$")           # word- at end of line
TABLE_LINE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\|")               # any pipe = possible table

def load_files():
    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    data = {}
    for f in files:
        try:
            data[f.name] = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        except Exception as e:
            data[f.name] = f"ERROR: {e}"
    return files, data

def inspect():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"Directory {MD_DIR} not found.")
        return

    files, data = load_files()
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR}\n")

    report = {
        "total_files": len(files),
        "naming_samples": [f.name for f in files[:10]],
        "naming_pattern": None,
        "files": {},
        "summary": {
            "files_with_page_marker": 0,
            "files_with_heading": 0,
            "files_with_likely_footer": 0,
            "files_with_hyphenation": 0,
            "files_with_table_lines": 0,
            "common_footer_candidates": [],
        },
    }

    # Guess naming pattern
    names = [f.name for f in files]
    if all(re.match(r"page_\d+\.md$", n) for n in names):
        report["naming_pattern"] = "page_N.md"
    elif all(re.match(r"\d+\.md$", n) for n in names):
        report["naming_pattern"] = "N.md"
    elif all(re.match(r".*_page\d+\.md$", n) for n in names):
        report["naming_pattern"] = "name_pageN.md"
    else:
        report["naming_pattern"] = "mixed/irregular"

    # Per-file inspection
    line_counter = Counter()
    for name, lines in data.items():
        if isinstance(lines, str):  # error
            report["files"][name] = {"error": lines}
            continue

        joined = "\n".join(lines)
        file_report = {
            "line_count": len(lines),
            "first_line": lines[0] if lines else "",
            "last_line": lines[-1] if lines else "",
            "has_page_marker": False,
            "page_marker_line": None,
            "has_heading": False,
            "heading_lines": [],
            "likely_footer_lines": [],
            "hyphenation_count": 0,
            "table_line_count": 0,
            "non_ascii_chars": sorted(set(ch for ch in joined if ord(ch) > 127)),
        }

        # Page marker detection
        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            stripped = line.strip()
            if any(p.match(stripped) for p in PAGE_NUM_PATTERNS):
                file_report["has_page_marker"] = True
                file_report["page_marker_line"] = stripped
                break

        # Heading detection
        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            if any(p.match(line.strip()) for p in HEADING_PATTERNS):
                file_report["has_heading"] = True
                file_report["heading_lines"].append(line.strip())

        # Hyphenation (broken words across lines)
        for i in range(len(lines) - 1):
            if HYPHENATION_PATTERN.search(lines[i]) and lines[i+1].strip() and lines[i+1].strip()[0].islower():
                file_report["hyphenation_count"] += 1

        # Table-ish lines
        file_report["table_line_count"] = sum(1 for l in lines if TABLE_LINE_PATTERN.search(l))

        # Track line frequency across all files (for footer detection)
        for l in lines:
            line_counter[l.strip()] += 1

        report["files"][name] = file_report

        if file_report["has_page_marker"]:
            report["summary"]["files_with_page_marker"] += 1
        if file_report["has_heading"]:
            report["summary"]["files_with_heading"] += 1
        if file_report["hyphenation_count"] > 0:
            report["summary"]["files_with_hyphenation"] += 1
        if file_report["table_line_count"] > 0:
            report["summary"]["files_with_table_lines"] += 1

    # Footer candidates: lines appearing in >70% of files
    threshold = max(1, int(len(files) * 0.7))
    footers = [line for line, count in line_counter.items() if count >= threshold and line]
    report["summary"]["common_footer_candidates"] = footers[:20]
    report["summary"]["files_with_likely_footer"] = sum(
        1 for fr in report["files"].values()
        if isinstance(fr, dict) and any(f in footers for f in [fr.get("first_line",""), fr.get("last_line","")])
    )

    # Print human-readable summary
    print("=" * 70)
    print("INSPECTION SUMMARY")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"Total files: {report['total_files']}")
    print(f"Naming pattern: {report['naming_pattern']}")
    print(f"Samples: {report['naming_samples'][:5]}")
    print()
    print(f"Files with page-number marker: {report['summary']['files_with_page_marker']}")
    print(f"Files with heading-like line:  {report['summary']['files_with_heading']}")
    print(f"Files with hyphenation breaks: {report['summary']['files_with_hyphenation']}")
    print(f"Files with table/pipe lines:   {report['summary']['files_with_table_lines']}")
    print(f"Likely repeated footer lines:  {report['summary']['common_footer_candidates'][:10]}")
    print()

    # Show a few examples of problems
    print("EXAMPLE FILE REPORTS (first 3):")
    for name in list(report["files"].keys())[:3]:
        fr = report["files"][name]
        print(f"\n--- {name} ---")
        print(f"  lines: {fr['line_count']}")
        print(f"  first: {fr['first_line'][:80]}")
        print(f"  last:  {fr['last_line'][:80]}")
        print(f"  page marker: {fr['page_marker_line']}")
        print(f"  headings: {fr['heading_lines'][:3]}")
        print(f"  hyphenation: {fr['hyphenation_count']}")
        print(f"  table lines: {fr['table_line_count']}")
        if fr["non_ascii_chars"]:
            print(f"  non-ascii: {fr['non_ascii_chars'][:10]}")

    REPORT_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"\nFull report saved to {REPORT_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    inspect()
```

### Expected Cleaning Problems

3. **Hyphenation** — words split across lines (e.g., `half-\nshaft`) need rejoining.
4. **Typos** — `Torque Specification s` (extra space) should be normalized.
5. **Whitespace artifacts** — multiple spaces, non-breaking spaces, stray newlines inside table cells.

### `clean_md.py`

```python
"""
Reusable markdown cleaning rules for vehicle service manual PDF extraction.
Designed to be imported by both the initial extraction and the resumable
remaining-pages extractor so output stays consistent across ./md/.
"""

import re
from typing import Optional

# Common header/footer patterns found in Ford-style workshop manuals
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    r"^SECTION\s+\d+-\d+:.+$",          # SECTION 205-04: Front Drive Halfshafts
    r"^SPECIFICATIONS\s*$",
    r"^\d{4}\s+F-?150\s+Workshop\s+Manual$",
    r"^Procedure\s+revision\s+date:\s+\d{2}/\d{2}/\d{4}$",
    r"^\d+\s*$",                          # lone page number
]

# Compile for speed
_HEADER_FOOTER_RE = re.compile("|".join(HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS), re.IGNORECASE)

# Hyphenation at end of line (word split across pages/columns)
_HYPHEN_LINEBREAK_RE = re.compile(r"(\w+)-\n(\w+)")

# Multiple blank lines
_MULTI_BLANK_RE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")

# Trailing spaces on lines
_TRAILING_SPACES_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)

# Non-breaking spaces and weird unicode spaces
_NBSP_RE = re.compile(r"[\u00a0\u2000-\u200a\u2028\u2029\u202f\u205f\u3000]")

# Em/en dash normalization (keep em-dash for null table cells, but normalize variants)
_DASH_VARIANTS_RE = re.compile(r"[\u2012\u2013\u2014\u2015]")


def remove_header_footer(text: str, page_num: Optional[int] = None) -> str:
    """Strip repeated running headers, footers, and lone page numbers."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    cleaned = []
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if not stripped:
            cleaned.append(line)
            continue
        if _HEADER_FOOTER_RE.match(stripped):
            continue
        cleaned.append(line)
    return "\n".join(cleaned)


def fix_hyphenation(text: str) -> str:
    """Rejoin words broken by hyphenation across line breaks."""
    return _HYPHEN_LINEBREAK_RE.sub(r"\1\2", text)


def normalize_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
    """Collapse NBSP variants, trailing spaces, and excessive blank lines."""
    text = _NBSP_RE.sub(" ", text)
    text = _TRAILING_SPACES_RE.sub("", text)
    text = _MULTI_BLANK_RE.sub("\n\n", text)
    return text.strip() + "\n"


def normalize_typos(text: str) -> str:
    """Fix known OCR/extraction typos in this manual."""
    text = text.replace("Torque Specification s", "Torque Specification")
    return text


def clean_page(text: str, page_num: Optional[int] = None) -> str:
    """
    Apply the full cleaning pipeline to one page of extracted markdown.
    Order matters: hyphenation first, then header/footer removal, then whitespace.
    """
    text = fix_hyphenation(text)
    text = remove_header_footer(text, page_num)
    text = normalize_typos(text)
    text = normalize_whitespace(text)
    return text
```

### `extract_remaining.py`

```python
"""
Resumable PyMuPDF extraction for the REMAINING pages of the service manual.
Skips any page that already has a corresponding .md file in ./md/.
Output format matches clean_md.py so the full manual is consistent.
"""

import fitz  # PyMuPDF
from pathlib import Path
import sys

# Add project root to path so we can import clean_md
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from clean_md import clean_page

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
PDF_PATH = Path("./data/2014_F-150_Workshop_Manual.pdf")  # update as needed
MD_DIR = Path("./md")
START_PAGE = 0          # 0-based index; set to Y+1 from your current state
END_PAGE = None         # None = extract to end of document
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def page_filename(page_number: int) -> str:
    """Zero-padded filename: page_042.md"""
    return f"page_{page_number:03d}.md"


def extract_page(doc: fitz.Document, page_index: int) -> str:
    """Extract raw text from a single PDF page using PyMuPDF."""
    page = doc[page_index]
    # 'text' gives plain text; 'html' or 'dict' could be used for table-aware extraction
    raw_text = page.get_text("text")
    return raw_text


def main():
    MD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if not PDF_PATH.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: PDF not found at {PDF_PATH}")
        sys.exit(1)

    doc = fitz.open(str(PDF_PATH))
    total_pages = doc.page_count
    end_page = END_PAGE if END_PAGE is not None else total_pages

    print(f"PDF has {total_pages} pages. Processing pages {START_PAGE} to {end_page - 1}.")

    skipped = 0
    extracted = 0

    for page_index in range(START_PAGE, end_page):
        out_name = page_filename(page_index)
        out_path = MD_DIR / out_name

        # Resumable: skip if already extracted
        if out_path.exists():
            skipped += 1
            continue

        raw_text = extract_page(doc, page_index)
        cleaned = clean_page(raw_text, page_num=page_index + 1)

        # Add a lightweight page header so source is traceable
        header = f"# Page {page_index + 1}\n\n"
        final_md = header + cleaned

        out_path.write_text(final_md, encoding="utf-8")
        extracted += 1

    print(f"Done. Extracted: {extracted}, Skipped (already exist): {skipped}.")
    doc.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Typical cleaning problems in service-manual PDFs include:
- Repeated running headers/footers on every page
- Section/revision-date lines that are not part of the article body
- Bullets converted to plain text or lost
- Hyphenated words split across lines (`high-\ntemperature`)
- Tables flattened into misaligned text
- Page numbers embedded in the text flow

### Reusable cleaning script (Step 1 fix)

Below is a production-ready cleaning script you can run against your existing `./md/` files once you share them. It is designed to be imported by the extraction script in Step 2 so both batches use identical rules.

```python
# clean_md.py
"""
Reusable markdown cleaning for service-manual PDF extraction.
Run: python clean_md.py --input ./md --output ./md_cleaned
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

# Patterns commonly found in workshop-manual PDFs
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    r"^\s*SECTION\s+\d+-\d+:\s+.*$",          # e.g. SECTION 205-04: Front Drive Halfsheets
    r"^\s*\d{4}\s+F-150\s+Workshop\s+Manual\s*$",
    r"^\s*Procedure\s+revision\s+date:\s+\d{2}/\d{2}/\d{4}\s*$",
    r"^\s*DESCRIPTION\s+AND\s+OPERATION\s*$",
    r"^\s*Page\s+\d+\s+of\s+\d+\s*$",
    r"^\s*\d+\s*$",                              # lone page number
]

HORIZONTAL_RULE = re.compile(r"^\s*[-]{3,}\s*$")
MULTIPLE_BLANKS = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")
HYPHEN_LINEBREAK = re.compile(r"(\w)-\n(\w)")
TRAILING_WHITESPACE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)


def clean_markdown(
    text: str,
    page_num: Optional[int] = None,
    source_name: Optional[str] = None,
) -> str:
    """
    Apply consistent cleaning rules to extracted markdown.
    Preserves headings (#, ##), lists, and paragraph structure.
    """
    lines = text.split("\n")
    cleaned_lines: list[str] = []

    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()

        # Skip blank lines (we'll re-normalize later)
        if not stripped:
            cleaned_lines.append("")
            continue

        # Skip repeated header/footer boilerplate
        if any(re.match(pat, stripped, re.IGNORECASE) for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS):
            continue

        # Skip standalone horizontal rules
        if HORIZONTAL_RULE.match(stripped):
            continue

        cleaned_lines.append(line)

    text = "\n".join(cleaned_lines)

    # Fix hyphenation across line breaks: "high-\ntemperature" -> "high-temperature"
    text = HYPHEN_LINEBREAK.sub(r"\1\2", text)

    # Remove trailing whitespace on each line
    text = TRAILING_WHITESPACE.sub("", text)

    # Collapse 3+ blank lines into 2 (single paragraph break)
    text = MULTIPLE_BLANKS.sub("\n\n", text)

    # Strip leading/trailing whitespace
    text = text.strip()

    # Append an invisible page anchor if we know the page number
    if page_num is not None:
        text = f"{text}\n\n<!-- Page {page_num} -->"

    return text


def clean_file(
    input_path: Path,
    output_path: Path,
    page_num: Optional[int] = None,
) -> None:
    raw = input_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    cleaned = clean_markdown(raw, page_num=page_num, source_name=input_path.name)
    output_path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Clean extracted markdown files.")
    parser.add_argument("--input", default="./md", help="Input directory of raw markdown")
    parser.add_argument("--output", default="./md_cleaned", help="Output directory")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    in_dir = Path(args.input)
    out_dir = Path(args.output)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    md_files = sorted(in_dir.glob("*.md"))
    if not md_files:
        print(f"No markdown files found in {in_dir}")
        return

    for md_file in md_files:
        # Attempt to parse page number from filename like page_042.md or 042.md
        m = re.search(r"(\d+)", md_file.stem)
        page_num = int(m.group(1)) if m else None
        clean_file(md_file, out_dir / md_file.name, page_num=page_num)
        print(f"Cleaned: {md_file.name} -> {out_dir / md_file.name}")

    print(f"\nDone. {len(md_files)} file(s) written to {out_dir}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### Step 1: Inspection Script

Save this as `inspect_md.py` and run it:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
inspect_md.py
Inspects ./md/ for naming, page/heading preservation, and common PDF-extraction
cleaning problems so we can write a targeted cleaning script next.
"""
import json
import re
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

MD_DIR = Path("./md")


def load_files():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR} not found.")
        return []
    files = []
    for f in sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md")):
        try:
            text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            files.append({"file": f.name, "path": f, "text": text})
        except Exception as e:
            files.append({"file": f.name, "error": str(e)})
    return files


def analyze(files):
    report = {
        "directory": str(MD_DIR),
        "total_files": len(files),
        "naming_samples": [],
        "page_numbers_in_filename": 0,
        "page_markers_in_content": 0,
        "headings_found": 0,
        "heading_levels": {},
        "empty_files": 0,
        "decode_errors": 0,
        "table_lines_total": 0,
        "files_with_inconsistent_tables": 0,
        "hyphenation_issues_total": 0,
        "repeated_header_footer_lines": {},
        "per_file": [],
    }

    line_counter = Counter()

    for item in files:
        fname = item["file"]
        report["naming_samples"].append(fname)

        if "error" in item:
            report["decode_errors"] += 1
            continue

        text = item["text"]

        if not text.strip():
            report["empty_files"] += 1

        # Page number hints
        if re.search(r"\d", fname):
            report["page_numbers_in_filename"] += 1
        if re.search(r"(?i)(page\s+\d+|\d+\s+of\s+\d+|^\s*\d+\s*$)", text, re.MULTILINE):
            report["page_markers_in_content"] += 1

        # Headings
        headings = re.findall(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.+)$", text, re.MULTILINE)
        report["headings_found"] += len(headings)
        for level, _ in headings:
            report["heading_levels"][level] = report["heading_levels"].get(level, 0) + 1

        # Track every line for header/footer detection
        for line in text.splitlines():
            stripped = line.strip()
            if stripped:
                line_counter[stripped] += 1

        # Tables
        table_rows = re.findall(r"^\s*\|.*\|\s*$", text, re.MULTILINE)
        report["table_lines_total"] += len(table_rows)
        pipe_counts = [row.count("|") for row in table_rows]
        if pipe_counts and len(set(pipe_counts)) > 1:
            report["files_with_inconsistent_tables"] += 1

        # Hyphenation: word- at end of line
        report["hyphenation_issues_total"] += len(re.findall(r"[A-Za-z]+-\s*\n", text))

        # Per-file sample
        report["per_file"].append({
            "file": fname,
            "chars": len(text),
            "lines": len(text.splitlines()),
            "first_300_chars": text[:300].replace("\n", " | "),
        })

    # Lines appearing in >50% of files are likely headers/footers
    threshold = max(1, len(files) // 2)
    repeated = {
        line: count
        for line, count in line_counter.items()
        if count >= threshold
    }
    report["repeated_header_footer_lines"] = dict(
        sorted(repeated.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:25]
    )

    return report


if __name__ == "__main__":
    files = load_files()
    report = analyze(files)
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2, default=str))
```

| Check | Why it matters for RAG |
|-------|------------------------|
| **Naming** (e.g. `page_001.md` vs `001.md` vs `205-04.md`) | Determines how the resumable extraction script maps PDF pages → md files. |
| **Page numbers in filename vs content** | Needed for `source_page` metadata and for skipping already-done pages. |
| **Headings (`#`, `##`)** | Needed for structure-aware chunking (section-aware splits). |
| **Repeated lines across files** | Likely running headers/footers (e.g. “2014 F-150 Workshop Manual”, revision dates) that should be stripped. |
| **Broken tables** (inconsistent `|` counts) | Service manuals are table-heavy; broken tables hurt retrieval and spec extraction. |
| **Hyphenation** (`word-\n`) | PDF line-break hyphens create false tokens and hurt embedding quality. |
| **Empty / decode-error files** | Must be handled before chunk
