### Step 1: Inspection & Cleaning Script

Save this as `scripts/inspect_clean_md.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Inspect and clean extracted markdown pages from a service manual.
Usage:
    python scripts/inspect_clean_md.py --md-dir ./md --inspect-only
    python scripts/inspect_clean_md.py --md-dir ./md --clean --out-dir ./md_clean
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Tuple

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configurable cleaning rules (edit these to match your manual's artifacts)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Lines that look like running headers / footers and should be stripped.
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^SECTION\s+\d+-\d+:.+", re.IGNORECASE),          # SECTION 205-04: ...
    re.compile(r"^2014\s+F-150\s+Workshop\s+Manual", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^Procedure\s+revision\s+date:", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^repair4less\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),               # watermark
    re.compile(r"^\d+\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),                        # lone page number
]

# Common section headings we want to preserve (add more as you discover them).
KNOWN_HEADINGS = [
    "Halfshaft",
    "Special Tool(s)",
    "Removal and Installation",
    "Inspection",
    "Specification",
    "Torque Specifications",
]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def extract_page_number(text: str, filename: str) -> int | None:
    """Try to find a page number in the text or filename."""
    m = re.search(r"page\s*(\d+)", text, re.IGNORECASE)
    if m:
        return int(m.group(1))
    m = re.search(r"(\d+)", Path(filename).stem)
    return int(m.group(1)) if m else None


def detect_issues(text: str) -> List[str]:
    issues: List[str] = []

    # Header/footer contamination
    for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS:
        if pat.search(text):
            issues.append(f"header_footer_match:{pat.pattern}")

    # Broken hyphenation across lines
    if re.search(r"-\n\w", text):
        issues.append("hyphenation_line_break")

    # Excessive whitespace
    if re.search(r"\n{4,}", text):
        issues.append("excessive_blank_lines")

    # Possible table wreckage: many pipes on one line or misaligned rows
    if text.count("|") > 0 and not re.search(r"^\s*[-|:\s]+\s*$", text, re.MULTILINE):
        issues.append("possible_broken_table")

    # Missing headings (heuristic: no markdown heading at all)
    if not re.search(r"^#{1,6}\s+", text, re.MULTILINE):
        issues.append("no_markdown_heading")

    return issues


def clean_text(raw: str, page_num: int | None = None) -> str:
    lines = raw.splitlines()
    cleaned_lines: List[str] = []

    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if not stripped:
            cleaned_lines.append("")
            continue

        # Drop header/footer lines
        if any(pat.match(stripped) for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS):
            continue

        cleaned_lines.append(stripped)

    text = "\n".join(cleaned_lines)

    # Fix hyphenation across line breaks: "trans-\nmission" -> "transmission"
    text = re.sub(r"(\w)-\n(\w)", r"\1\2", text)

    # Collapse 3+ blank lines to 2
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)

    # Add YAML frontmatter with page number if we have one
    if page_num is not None:
        frontmatter = f"---\npage: {page_num}\nsource: service_manual.pdf\n---\n\n"
        text = frontmatter + text

    return text.strip() + "\n"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def inspect(md_dir: Path) -> None:
    files = sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {md_dir}\n")
    print(f"{'File':<30} {'Pages':<8} {'Issues':<40} {'Headings'}")
    print("-" * 100)

    for f in files:
        raw = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
        page = extract_page_number(raw, f.name)
        issues = detect_issues(raw)
        headings = re.findall(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.+)$", raw, re.MULTILINE)
        heading_str = "; ".join(f"{h[0]} {h[1]}" for h in headings[:3])
        if len(headings) > 3:
            heading_str += " ..."

        print(f"{f.name:<30} {str(page):<8} {', '.join(issues):<40} {heading_str}")


def clean(md_dir: Path, out_dir: Path) -> None:
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    files = sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Cleaning {len(files)} files -> {out_dir}")

    for f in files:
        raw = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
        page = extract_page_number(raw, f.name)
        cleaned = clean_text(raw, page)
        out_path = out_dir / f.name
        out_path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"  {f.name} -> {out_path} (page={page})")


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Inspect/clean extracted manual markdown.")
    parser.add_argument("--md-dir", default="./md", help="Directory with raw markdown files.")
    parser.add_argument("--out-dir", default="./md_clean", help="Output directory for cleaned files.")
    parser.add_argument("--inspect-only", action="store_true", help="Only report findings, don't clean.")
    parser.add_argument("--clean", action="store_true", help="Run cleaning and write output.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    md_dir = Path(args.md_dir)
    if not md_dir.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {md_dir} does not exist.", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

    if args.inspect_only:
        inspect(md_dir)
    elif args.clean:
        clean(md_dir, Path(args.out_dir))
    else:
        parser.print_help()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### Anticipated cleaning problems in `./md/`
1. **Headers/footers** – repeating manual title, page numbers, or section names on every page.
2. **Broken tables** – table rows split across page boundaries, producing malformed Markdown tables.
3. **Hyphenation** – words split across lines (`brake-\nflexible`) that need rejoining.
4. **Watermarks** – `repair4less` or similar overlay text.
5. **Orphan figure labels** – codes like `A0049442` floating without context.
6. **Lost hyperlinks** – cross-references like `Section 100-02` may become plain text.

## Reusable cleaning script

Save this as `scripts/clean_md.py`. It applies deterministic rules (no manual editing) and can be re-run safely.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Reusable Markdown cleaner for service-manual extraction.
Removes headers/footers, watermarks, page-number-only lines,
fixes hyphenation, and normalizes whitespace while preserving
headings, lists, tables, and code blocks.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
MD_DIR = Path("./md")
WATERMARKS = ["repair4less", "DRAFT", "CONFIDENTIAL"]
# Lines that are only a page number (1–4 digits)
PAGE_NUMBER_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$")
# Hyphenation at end of line: word-\nword  ->  wordword
HYPHEN_RE = re.compile(r"(\w)-\n(\w)")
# Collapse 3+ blank lines into 2
BLANK_LINES_RE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")


def clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    # 1. Remove watermark lines (case-insensitive)
    for wm in WATERMARKS:
        text = re.sub(re.escape(wm), "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)

    # 2. Remove standalone page-number lines
    lines = text.split("\n")
    kept = [ln for ln in lines if not PAGE_NUMBER_RE.match(ln)]
    text = "\n".join(kept)

    # 3. Fix hyphenation across line breaks
    text = HYPHEN_RE.sub(r"\1\2", text)

    #

### Proxy Findings (from the sample page image)

Based on the page shown (steps 12–15, CV shaft / halfshaft removal), your extracted markdown likely contains:

| Element | Observation in image | Likely extraction issue |

| **Naming / format** | Unknown (you said `./md/`) | Probably `page_001.md`, `page_045.md`, etc. |
| **Page numbers** | Not visible in the image crop | May appear as standalone digits in headers/footers |

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Inspect ./md/ files: naming, page metadata, headings, and cleaning issues."""
import re
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

def inspect():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR.resolve()} does not exist.")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    if not files:
        print(f"No markdown files found in {MD_DIR.resolve()}")
        return

    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR.resolve()}\n")
    print(f"{'File':<24} {'Lines':>6} {'FrontMatter':>12} {'PageMeta':>9} {'FirstHeading'}")
    print("-" * 90)

    issues = Counter()
    examples = {}

    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        lines = text.splitlines()

        has_frontmatter = lines[0].strip() == "---"
        page_meta = bool(re.search(r"^page:\s*\d+", text, re.MULTILINE)) if has_frontmatter else False
        first_heading = next((l.strip() for l in lines if l.strip().startswith("#")), None)

        # Issue detection
        standalone_digits = sum(1 for l in lines if re.fullmatch(r"\d{1,4}", l.strip()))
        hyphen_breaks = len(re.findall(r"\w-\n\w", text))
        header_footer_text = sum(
            1 for l in lines
            if re.search(r"(Copyright|All rights reserved|Printed in|^Page\s)", l, re.IGNORECASE)
        )
        blank_line_runs = len(re.findall(r"\n{4,}", text))

        if standalone_digits:
            issues["standalone_digits"] += standalone_digits
            examples.setdefault("standalone_digits", f.name)
        if hyphen_breaks:
            issues["hyphenation"] += hyphen_breaks
            examples.setdefault("hyphenation", f.name)
        if header_footer_text:
            issues["header_footer_text"] += header_footer_text
            examples.setdefault("header_footer_text", f.name)
        if blank_line_runs:
            issues["excessive_blank_lines"] += blank_line_runs
            examples.setdefault("excessive_blank_lines", f.name)

        print(
            f"{f.name:<24} {len(lines):>6} {str(has_frontmatter):>12} "
            f"{str(page_meta):>9} {first_heading!r}"
        )

    print("-" * 90)
    print("\nIssue summary:")
    if issues:
        for k, v in issues.most_common():
            print(f"  - {k}: {v} (e.g., {examples.get(k)})")
    else:
        print("  No common cleaning issues detected.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    inspect()
```

### 2. Reusable inspection script

Save this as `inspect_md.py` in your project root and run it. It will scan `./md/`, report naming/format, detect page numbers/headings, and flag common cleaning problems.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
inspect_md.py
Inspect existing markdown files in ./md/ and report:
- naming convention
- page number / heading preservation
- common cleaning problems (headers/footers, broken tables, hyphenation)
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import json
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

# Patterns for common boilerplate in Ford workshop manuals
BOILERPLATE_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"2014\s+F-150\s+Workshop\s+Manual", re.I),
    re.compile(r"Procedure\s+revision\s+date:", re.I),
    re.compile(r"SECTION\s+\d+-\d+:", re.I),
    re.compile(r"DISASSEMBLY\s+AND\s+ASSEMBLY", re.I),
]

PAGE_NUMBER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$"),  # lone number line
    re.compile(r"Page\s+\d+", re.I),
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d+\s+of\s+\d+\s*$", re.I),
]

HYPHENATION_PATTERN = re.compile(r"-\s*\n\s*[a-z]")  # word- at end of line, lowercase next


def analyze_file(path: Path) -> dict:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    lines = text.splitlines()

    report = {
        "file": path.name,
        "size_bytes": path.stat().st_size,
        "n_lines": len(lines),
        "n_chars": len(text),
        "headings": [],
        "page_number_lines": [],
        "boilerplate_lines": [],
        "table_lines": 0,
        "broken_table_rows": [],
        "hyphenation_issues": 0,
        "sample_lines": lines[:5],
    }

    # Headings
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        if re.match(r"^#{1,6}\s+", line):
            report["headings"].append({"line_no": i + 1, "text": line.strip()})

    # Page numbers
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        if any(p.search(line) for p in PAGE_NUMBER_PATTERNS):
            report["page_number_lines"].append({"line_no": i + 1, "text": line.strip()})

    # Boilerplate
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        if any(p.search(line) for p in BOILERPLATE_PATTERNS):
            report["boilerplate_lines"].append({"line_no": i + 1, "text": line.strip()})

    # Tables
    table_lines = [l for l in lines if l.strip().startswith("|")]
    report["table_lines"] = len(table_lines)

    # Check pipe consistency within contiguous table blocks
    in_table = False
    expected_pipes = None
    table_start = 0
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        stripped = line.strip()
        if stripped.startswith("|"):
            if not in_table:
                in_table = True
                table_start = i
                expected_pipes = stripped.count("|")
            else:
                if stripped.count("|") != expected_pipes:
                    report["broken_table_rows"].append({
                        "line_no": i + 1,
                        "text": stripped[:120],
                        "pipe_count": stripped.count("|"),
                        "expected": expected_pipes,
                    })
        else:
            in_table = False
            expected_pipes = None

    # Hyphenation
    report["hyphenation_issues"] = len(HYPHENATION_PATTERN.findall(text))

    return report


def main():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR} does not exist.")
        return

    md_files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    if not md_files:
        print(f"No .md files found in {MD_DIR}")
        return

    print(f"Found {len(md_files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR}\n")

    all_reports = []
    for f in md_files:
        r = analyze_file(f)
        all_reports.append(r)

    # Cross-file boilerplate detection (lines repeated in >50% of files)
    line_counter = Counter()
    for r in all_reports:
        for b in r["boilerplate_lines"]:
            line_counter[b["text"]] += 1

    repeated_boilerplate = {
        text: count for text, count in line_counter.items() if count > len(md_files) * 0.5
    }

    # Print per-file summary
    print(f"{'File':<30} {'Lines':>6} {'Chars':>8} {'Headings':>9} {'Pages':>6} {'Boiler':>8} {'TableLns':>9} {'BrokenTbl':>10} {'Hyphen':>7}")
    for r in all_reports:
        print(
            f"{r['file']:<30} {r['n_lines']:>6} {r['n_chars']:>8} "
            f"{len(r['headings']):>9} {len(r['page_number_lines']):>6} "
            f"{len(r['boilerplate_lines']):>8} {r['table_lines']:>9} "
            f"{len(r['broken_table_rows']):>10} {r['hyphenation_issues']:>7}"
        )

    print("\n--- Repeated boilerplate (appears in >50% of files) ---")
    if repeated_boilerplate:
        for text, count in repeated_boilerplate.most_common():
            print(f"  [{count}/{len(md_files)}] {text[:100]}")
    else:
        print("  None detected.")

    print("\n--- Per-file details (first 3 files) ---")
    for r in all_reports[:3]:
        print(f"\n### {r['file']}")
        if r["headings"]:
            print("  Headings:")
            for h in r["headings"][:10]:
                print(f"    L{h['line_no']}: {h['text'][:100]}")
        if r["page_number_lines"]:
            print("  Page-number-like lines:")
            for p in r["page_number_lines"][:5]:
                print(f"    L{p['line_no']}: {p['text'][:100]}")
        if r["broken_table_rows"]:
            print("  Broken table rows:")
            for b in r["broken_table_rows"][:5]:
                print(f"    L{b['line_no']} (pipes={b['pipe_count']}, expected={b['expected']}): {b['text'][:100]}")
        if r["hyphenation_issues"]:
            print(f"  Hyphenation issues: {r['hyphenation_issues']}")
        print("  First 3 lines:")
        for s in r["sample_lines"][:3]:
            print(f"    {s[:120]}")

    # Save JSON report
    out = {
        "n_files": len(md_files),
        "repeated_boilerplate": repeated_boilerplate,
        "files": all_reports,
    }
    Path("inspection_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print("\nSaved full report to inspection_report.json")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### Reusable cleaning script

Below is `clean_md.py`. It implements the cleaning rules that should be applied **both** to the existing files in `./md/` and to any newly extracted pages, so the whole manual ends up consistent.

```python
# clean_md.py
"""
Reusable markdown cleaning for vehicle service manual pages.
Applies the same rules to existing ./md/ files and newly extracted pages.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

# Common noise patterns found in service-manual PDFs
WATERMARK_PATTERNS = [
    r"\brepair4less\b",
    r"\bwww\.repair4less\.com\b",
]

# Figure / diagram codes that often appear as isolated OCR artifacts
# e.g. DE1341-A, A0013384, GE1177-A
FIGURE_CODE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\n\s*\b[A-Z]{1,3}\d{3,}[-A-Z0-9]*\b\s*\n")

# Hyphenation across line breaks: "grease-\ncontaminated" -> "greasecontaminated"
HYPHEN_LINEBREAK = re.compile(r"(\w)-\n(\w)")

# Multiple blank lines
MULTI_BLANK = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")

# Trailing/leading whitespace on lines
TRAILING_WS = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)


def clean_text(raw: str, page_num: Optional[int] = None) -> str:
    """Clean raw extracted text and return standardized markdown."""
    text = raw

    # 1. Remove watermarks
    for pat in WATERMARK_PATTERNS:
        text = re.sub(pat, "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)

    # 2. Remove isolated figure codes on their own lines (noise)
    text = FIGURE_CODE_PATTERN.sub("\n", text)

    # 3. Fix hyphenation across line breaks
    text = HYPHEN_LINEBREAK.sub(r"\1\2", text)

    # 4. Normalize intra-line whitespace
    text = TRAILING_WS.sub("", text)
    text = re.sub(r"[ \t]+", " ", text)

    # 5. Collapse excessive blank lines
    text = MULTI_BLANK.sub("\n\n", text)

    # 6. Inject page frontmatter if requested
    if page_num is not None:
        text = f"---\npage: {page_num}\n---\n\n{text.strip()}"

    return text.strip() + "\n"


def clean_file(path: Path, page_num: Optional[int] = None) -> str:
    """Read, clean, and overwrite a single markdown file in place."""
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    cleaned = clean_text(raw, page_num=page_num)
    path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
    return cleaned


def clean_directory(md_dir: Path | str = "./md") -> list[Path]:
    """Clean every .md file in a directory. Returns list of cleaned files."""
    md_dir = Path(md_dir)
    files = sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))
    for f in files:
        # Try to infer page number from filename like page_010.md
        m = re.search(r"(\d+)", f.stem)
        page = int(m.group(1)) if m else None
        clean_file(f, page_num=page)
    return files
```

### Extraction script for remaining pages (Step 1b / Step 2)

This script uses **PyMuPDF**, processes **only** pages that don't already have an `.md` file, applies the **same** `clean_text` rules, and writes output to `./md/` with the same naming convention.

```python
# extract_remaining.py
"""
Extract remaining PDF pages to ./md/ using PyMuPDF.
Resumable: skips pages that already have a markdown file.
Uses the same cleaning rules as clean_md.py.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import fitz  # PyMuPDF
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys

# Make sure clean_md is importable
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
from clean_md import clean_text

PDF_PATH = Path("service_manual.pdf")   # <-- update to your actual PDF path
MD_DIR = Path("./md")
MD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Output naming: page_001.md, page_002.md, ...
PAGE_RE = re.compile(r"page_(\d+)\.md$")


def existing_pages() -> set[int]:
    """Return set of page numbers already extracted."""
    pages = set()
    for f in MD_DIR.glob("page_*.md"):
        m = PAGE_RE.search(f.name)
        if m:
            pages.add(int(m.group(1)))
    return pages


def extract_page_text(page: fitz.Page) -> str:
    """Extract text from a PyMuPDF page. Preserves reading order."""
    # "text" gives plain text in reading order; use "blocks" if you need layout
    return page.get_text("text")


def main() -> None:
    if not PDF_PATH.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: PDF not found at {PDF_PATH.resolve()}")
        sys.exit(1)

    doc = fitz.open(str(PDF_PATH))
    done = existing_pages()
    total = len(doc)

    print(f"PDF has {total} pages. Already extracted: {len(done)}")

    for i in range(total):
        page_num = i + 1  # 1-indexed
        if page_num in done:
            continue

        page = doc[i]
        raw = extract_page_text(page)
        cleaned = clean_text(raw, page_num=page_num)

        out_path = MD_DIR / f"page_{page_num:03d}.md"
        out_path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"[OK] page {page_num:03d} -> {out_path.name}")

    print("Extraction complete.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### What the script does

| Check | How it’s detected |
|-------|-------------------|
| **Naming / format** | Lists all `*.md` files, size, line count |
| **Page numbers preserved** | Looks for page markers (e.g. `Page 42`, `--- 42 ---`) |
| **Headings preserved** | Finds Markdown headings (`#`, `##`, etc.) |
| **Headers / footers** | Flags short repeated lines at top/bottom of pages |
| **Broken tables** | Looks for misaligned pipe tables or orphaned `|` characters |
| **Hyphenation** | Flags words split at line breaks (`word-\nword`) |
| **Whitespace artifacts** | Multiple spaces, trailing spaces, excessive blank lines |

### Step 1 script: `step1_inspect_clean.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Step 1: Inspect and clean existing markdown files in ./md/
Usage: python step1_inspect_clean.py
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
from collections import Counter

MD_DIR = Path("./md")
CLEAN_DIR = Path("./md_cleaned")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Cleaning rules (shared with Step 2 so both halves of the manual match)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
PAGE_MARKER_RE = re.compile(r'(?:^|\n)\s*(?:page\s*)?\d{1,4}\s*(?:\n|$)', re.IGNORECASE)
LONE_NUMBER_RE = re.compile(r'^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$')
HYPHEN_BREAK_RE = re.compile(r'(\w)-\n(\w)')
MULTISPACE_RE = re.compile(r'[ \t]{2,}')
EXCESS_BLANKS_RE = re.compile(r'\n{3,}')
PIPE_TABLE_RE = re.compile(r'^\s*\|.*\|\s*$', re.MULTILINE)


def list_md_files(md_dir: Path):
    return sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))


def inspect(path: Path) -> dict:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    lines = text.splitlines()
    headings = re.findall(r'^#{1,6}\s+.+', text, re.MULTILINE)
    return {
        "name": path.name,
        "size": path.stat().st_size,
        "lines": len(lines),
        "chars": len(text),
        "has_page_marker": bool(PAGE_MARKER_RE.search(text)),
        "heading_count": len(headings),
        "headings": headings[:5],  # show first 5 in report
        "first_line": lines[0] if lines else "",
        "last_line": lines[-1] if lines else "",
    }


def detect_issues(text: str) -> list[str]:
    issues = []
    lines = text.splitlines()

    # Lone page numbers
    lone = [l for l in lines if LONE_NUMBER_RE.match(l)]
    if lone:
        issues.append(f"lone page numbers: {len(lone)}")

    # Hyphenation at line breaks
    hyph = [l for l in lines if re.search(r'-\s*$', l)]
    if hyph:
        issues.append(f"hyphenated line-breaks: {len(hyph)}")

    # Possible header/footer: very short lines repeated at start/end
    short_lines = [l.strip() for l in lines if 0 < len(l.strip()) <= 40]
    if short_lines:
        counts = Counter(short_lines)
        repeated = [k for k, v in counts.items() if v > 1 and len(k) > 2]
        if repeated:
            issues.append(f"repeated short lines (header/footer?): {repeated[:5]}")

    # Broken tables: pipe lines that don't look like a clean table
    pipes = PIPE_TABLE_RE.findall(text)
    if pipes and not re.search(r'^\s*\|[-:\s|]+\|\s*$', text, re.MULTILINE):
        issues.append(f"possible broken table rows: {len(pipes)}")

    # Whitespace
    if MULTISPACE_RE.search(text):
        issues.append("multiple spaces")
    if EXCESS_BLANKS_RE.search(text):
        issues.append("excess blank lines")

    return issues


def clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    # Normalize line endings
    text = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n")

    # Drop standalone page-number lines
    lines = text.split("\n")
    lines = [l for l in lines if not LONE_NUMBER_RE.match(l)]
    text = "\n".join(lines)

    # Join hyphenated words split across lines: "grease-\nline" -> "greaseline"
    # (Conservative: removes the hyphen and newline only.)
    text = HYPHEN_BREAK_RE.sub(r"\1\2", text)

    # Collapse multiple spaces/tabs to one (inside lines)
    text = MULTISPACE_RE.sub(" ", text)

    # Strip trailing whitespace per line
    text = "\n".join(l.rstrip() for l in text.split("\n"))

    # Collapse 3+ blank lines to 2
    text = EXCESS_BLANKS_RE.sub("\n\n", text)

    return text.strip() + "\n"


def main():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR} not found. Create it or point MD_DIR at your markdown folder.")
        return

    files = list_md_files(MD_DIR)
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR}\n")

    header = f"{'File':<28} {'Lines':<7} {'Chars':<8} {'PageMarker':<12} {'Headings':<10} {'Issues'}"
    print(header)
    print("-" * len(header))

    CLEAN_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    for f in files:
        info = inspect(f)
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        issues = detect_issues(text)

        print(
            f"{info['name']:<28} "
            f"{info['lines']:<7} "
            f"{info['chars']:<8} "
            f"{str(info['has_page_marker']):<12} "
            f"{info['heading_count']:<10} "
            f{'; '.join(issues) if issues else 'none'}"
        )

        cleaned = clean_text(text)
        out_path = CLEAN_DIR / f.name
        out_path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")

    print(f"\nCleaned files written to: {CLEAN_DIR.resolve()}")
    print("Review the 'Issues' column above before proceeding to Step 2.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## Step 1: Inspection script

Save this as `scripts/inspect_md.py` and run it from the project root.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Inspect existing markdown files in ./md/.
Reports naming, format, page/heading preservation, and common cleaning problems.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

# Patterns to detect common artifacts
PAGE_MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"(?:^|\s)(?:page\s*)?(\d{1,4})(?:\s|$)", re.IGNORECASE)
HEADING_RE = re.compile(r"^\s{0,3}#{1,6}\s+.+", re.MULTILINE)
HYPHENATION_RE = re.compile(r"-\n\s*")
TABLE_LINE_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*\|.*\|\s*$", re.MULTILINE)
WHITESPACE_ISSUE_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]{2,}|\r")
FOOTER_LIKE_RE = re.compile(
    r"(repair4less|page\s+\d+|©|copyright|all rights reserved|manual\s*no\.?)",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)


def inspect_file(path: Path) -> dict:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    lines = text.splitlines()

    report = {
        "file": path.name,
        "size_bytes": path.stat().st_size,
        "lines": len(lines),
        "chars": len(text),
        "headings": len(HEADING_RE.findall(text)),
        "page_markers_found": PAGE_MARKER_RE.findall(text)[:5],
        "hyphenation_breaks": len(HYPHENATION_RE.findall(text)),
        "table_like_lines": len(TABLE_LINE_RE.findall(text)),
        "whitespace_issues": len(WHITESPACE_ISSUE_RE.findall(text)),
        "footer_like_lines": len(FOOTER_LIKE_RE.findall(text)),
        "first_line": lines[0].strip()[:100] if lines else "",
        "last_line": lines[-1].strip()[:100] if lines else "",
    }
    return report


def main() -> None:
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"Directory {MD_DIR} not found.")
        return

    md_files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    if not md_files:
        print(f"No markdown files found in {MD_DIR}")
        return

    print(f"Found {len(md_files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR}\n")
    print("=" * 100)

    all_reports = []
    for f in md_files:
        r = inspect_file(f)
        all_reports.append(r)
        print(f"\nFile: {r['file']}")
        print(f"  Size: {r['size_bytes']} bytes | Lines: {r['lines']} | Chars: {r['chars']}")
        print(f"  Headings: {r['headings']}")
        print(f"  Page markers (sample): {r['page_markers_found']}")
        print(f"  Hyphenation breaks: {r['hyphenation_breaks']}")
        print(f"  Table-like lines: {r['table_like_lines']}")
        print(f"  Whitespace issues: {r['whitespace_issues']}")
        print(f"  Footer-like lines: {r['footer_like_lines']}")
        print(f"  First line: {r['first_line']}")
        print(f"  Last line:  {r['last_line']}")

    # Summary
    print("\n" + "=" * 100)
    print("SUMMARY")
    print("=" * 100)
    total_files = len(all_reports)
    total_hyph = sum(r["hyphenation_breaks"] for r in all_reports)
    total_tables = sum(r["table_like_lines"] for r in all_reports)
    total_ws = sum(r["whitespace_issues"] for r in all_reports)
    total_footer = sum(r["footer_like_lines"] for r in all_reports)
    files_with_headings = sum(1 for r in all_reports if r["headings"] > 0)
    files_with_page_markers = sum(1 for r in all_reports if r["page_markers_found"])

    print(f"Total files: {total_files}")
    print(f"Files with headings: {files_with_headings}/{total_files}")
    print(f"Files with page-number markers: {files_with_page_markers}/{total_files}")
    print(f"Total hyphenation breaks: {total_hyph}")
    print(f"Total table-like lines: {total_tables}")
    print(f"Total whitespace issues: {total_ws}")
    print(f"Total footer/header-like lines: {total_footer}")

    # Naming pattern guess
    names = [r["file"] for r in all_reports]
    print(f"\nSample names: {names[:5]} ... {names[-3:]}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### `scripts/clean_md.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Reusable cleaning script for extracted manual markdown.
Applies the same rules to existing files and newly extracted pages.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION — adjust these after running inspect_md.py
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Regexes for headers/footers to strip (one per line, matched anywhere in line)
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    r"repair4less",
    r"page\s+\d+",
    r"©.*",
    r"all rights reserved",
    r"manual\s*no\.?",
]

# If page numbers appear as standalone lines, remove them
STANDALONE_PAGE_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$", re.MULTILINE)

HYPHENATION_RE = re.compile(r"(\w)-\n(\w)")  # join hyphenated word breaks
MULTISPACE_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]{2,}")
TRAILING_WS_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)
MULTIPLE_NEWLINES_RE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")


def clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    # Remove header/footer lines
    for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS:
        text = re.sub(pat, "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)

    # Remove standalone page numbers
    text = STANDALONE_PAGE_RE.sub("", text)

    # Fix hyphenation across line breaks: "trans-\nmission" -> "transmission"
    text = HYPHENATION_RE.sub(r"\1\2", text)

    # Normalize whitespace
    text = TRAILING_WS_RE.sub("", text)
    text = MULTISPACE_RE.sub(" ", text)
    text = MULTIPLE_NEWLINES_RE.sub("\n\n", text)

    return text.strip() + "\n"


def clean_file(path: Path) -> None:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    cleaned = clean_text(text)
    if cleaned != text:
        path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"Cleaned: {path.name}")
    else:
        print(f"OK:      {path.name}")


def main() -> None:
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"{MD_DIR} not found.")
        return
    md_files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    for f in md_files:
        clean_file(f)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## 2. Reusable cleaning script

Save this as `scripts/clean_md.py`. It applies the same rules to existing files and to any new files produced by the extraction script in Step 2.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
clean_md.py
Reusable cleaning script for extracted service-manual markdown.

Rules:
  1. Remove standalone page numbers
  2. Remove footer watermarks (e.g., "repair4less")
  3. Remove isolated figure/artifact labels (e.g., "ND055207")
  4. Fix hyphenation broken across line breaks
  5. Normalize whitespace (tabs, multiple spaces, excessive blank lines)
  6. Preserve headings, lists, and inline tables as-is
"""

import re
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

# Patterns to drop entirely (case-insensitive where appropriate)
DROP_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^\s*repair4less\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),          # watermark
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$"),                              # standalone page number
    re.compile(r"^\s*ND\d{6}\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),              # figure label ND055207
    re.compile(r"^\s*Figure\s*\d+[\.:]?\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),   # orphaned "Figure 3"
]

HYPHEN_BREAK = re.compile(r"(\w+)-\n(\w+)")                    # grease-\nase -> greasease? see below
MULTI_SPACE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+")
MULTI_NEWLINE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")


def fix_hyphenation(text: str) -> str:
    """
    Rejoin words split by a hyphen at end of line.
    Conservative: only rejoin if both sides look like word characters.
    """
    return HYPHEN_BREAK.sub(r"\1\2", text)


def drop_artifacts(text: str) -> str:
    lines = text.split("\n")
    kept = []
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if any(pat.match(stripped) for pat in DROP_PATTERNS):
            continue
        kept.append(line)
    return "\n".join(kept)


def normalize_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
    text = MULTI_SPACE.sub(" ", text)
    text = MULTI_NEWLINE.sub("\n\n", text)
    return text.strip()


def clean_markdown(text: str) -> str:
    text = drop_artifacts(text)
    text = fix_hyphenation(text)
    text = normalize_whitespace(text)
    return text


def process_file(path: Path, dry_run: bool = False) -> bool:
    """Returns True if file was changed (or would be changed)."""
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    cleaned = clean_markdown(raw)
    changed = cleaned != raw

    if changed and not dry_run:
        path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")

    return changed


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Clean extracted markdown files in ./md/")
    parser.add_argument("md_dir", nargs="?", default="./md", help="Directory containing .md files")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Report changes without writing")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    md_path = Path(args.md_dir)
    if not md_path.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: directory not found: {md_path.resolve()}")
        return

    files = sorted(md_path.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {md_path.resolve()}\n")

    changed_count = 0
    for f in files:
        changed = process_file(f, dry_run=args.dry_run)
        if changed:
            changed_count += 1
            print(f"[{'DRY-RUN' if args.dry_run else 'OK'}] {'would update' if args.dry_run else 'updated'}: {f.name}")
        else:
            print(f"[SKIP] no changes: {f.name}")

    print(f"\n{changed_count} file(s) {'would be ' if args.dry_run else ''}modified.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### How to run
```bash
# See what would change without writing
python scripts/clean_md.py ./md --dry-run

# Apply cleaning
python scripts/clean_md.py ./md
```

### Step 1: `scripts/step1_inspect_clean.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Step 1: Inspect and clean existing Markdown extractions in ./md/.

What it does:
  1. Lists every .md file in ./md/
  2. Reports: filename, size, line count, page markers, headings, detected issues
  3. Applies reusable cleaning rules:
     - Remove form feeds (\f)
     - Fix end-of-line hyphenation (speci-\nfication -> specification)
     - Normalize intra-line whitespace
     - Strip trailing whitespace per line
     - Collapse 3+ blank lines to 2
  4. Writes cleaned files back to ./md/ (only if changed)
  5. Emits a JSON report to ./reports/

Run:  python scripts/step1_inspect_clean.py

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from datetime import datetime

MD_DIR = Path("./md")
REPORT_DIR = Path("./reports")

FORM_FEED = re.compile(r"\f")
HYPHEN_NEWLINE = re.compile(r"(\w)-\n(\w)")          # speci-\nfication
MULTI_SPACE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+")
TRAILING_WS = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)
MULTI_BLANK = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")


def clean_markdown(text: str) -> str:
    """Apply the standard cleaning pipeline. Returns cleaned text."""
    text = FORM_FEED.sub("\n", text)
    text = HYPHEN_NEWLINE.sub(r"\1\2", text)
    text = MULTI_SPACE.sub(" ", text)
    text = TRAILING_WS.sub("", text)
    text = MULTI_BLANK.sub("\n\n", text)
    return text.strip() + "\n"

PAGE_MARKER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^\s*#+\s*Page\s+(\d+)\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),   # # Page 42

]

HEADING_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*(#{1,6})\s+(.+)$")


def inspect_file(path: Path) -> dict:

### 2. Reusable inspection script

Save this as `inspect_md.py` in your project root. It scans `./md/`, reports naming/format, checks for page numbers and headings, and flags common cleaning problems.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Step 1: Inspect existing ./md/ files.
Reports naming, page/heading preservation, and cleaning problems.
Outputs a markdown report to ./inspection_report.md
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import collections
import sys

MD_DIR = Path("./md")
REPORT_PATH = Path("./inspection_report.md")

# Patterns
PAGE_NUM_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^\s*#?\s*Page\s+(\d+)", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*(\d{1,3})\.\s"),          # "23. NOTICE: ..."
    re.compile(r"^\s*(\d{1,3})\s*[-|]\s*"),    # "23 - ..."
]
HEADING_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^#{1,6}\s+.+"),               # Markdown headings
    re.compile(r"^[A-Z][A-Z\s]{3,}$"),         # ALL CAPS headings
]
HYPHENATION_PATTERN = re.compile(r"-\n\w")   # word-\nword
TABLE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^\s*\|")        # lines starting with |
REPEATED_LINE_THRESHOLD = 0.5                # flag if >50% of files share a line


def read_md_files(md_dir: Path):
    files = sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))
    data = []
    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        data.append({"path": f, "name": f.name, "text": text, "lines": text.splitlines()})
    return data


def detect_page_number(entry):
    """Try to find a page number in the first 10 lines or via numbered notices."""
    for line in entry["lines"][:15]:
        for pat in PAGE_NUM_PATTERNS:
            m = pat.search(line)
            if m:
                return int(m.group(1)), line.strip()
    # fallback: look for "Page X of Y" anywhere
    for line in entry["lines"]:
        m = re.search(r"page\s+(\d+)\s+of\s+(\d+)", line, re.IGNORECASE)
        if m:
            return int(m.group(1)), line.strip()
    return None, None


def detect_headings(entry):
    headings = []
    for line in entry["lines"]:
        for pat in HEADING_PATTERNS:
            if pat.search(line):
                headings.append(line.strip())
                break
    return headings


def find_hyphenation(entry):
    return [line for line in entry["lines"] if HYPHENATION_PATTERN.search(line)]


def find_table_artifacts(entry):
    return [line for line in entry["lines"] if TABLE_PATTERN.search(line)]


def find_repeated_lines(entries):
    """Find lines that appear in many files (likely headers/footers)."""
    counter = collections.Counter()
    for e in entries:
        # normalize whitespace for comparison
        norm = {re.sub(r"\s+", " ", l).strip() for l in e["lines"] if l.strip()}
        for line in norm:
            counter[line] += 1
    total = len(entries)
    repeated = [
        (line, count, round(count / total, 2))
        for line, count in counter.items()
        if count / total >= REPEATED_LINE_THRESHOLD and len(line) > 3
    ]
    return sorted(repeated, key=lambda x: -x[1])


def main():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR} not found. Create it or point MD_DIR elsewhere.", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

    entries = read_md_files(MD_DIR)
    if not entries:
        print(f"WARNING: No .md files found in {MD_DIR}")
        sys.exit(0)

    report_lines = []
    report_lines.append("# MD File Inspection Report")
    report_lines.append(f"\nDirectory: `{MD_DIR.resolve()}`")
    report_lines.append(f"Total files: {len(entries)}")

    # --- Naming ---
    report_lines.append("\n## 1. Naming Convention")
    names = [e["name"] for e in entries]
    report_lines.append("\n".join(f"- `{n}`" for n in names[:50]))
    if len(names) > 50:
        report_lines.append(f"... and {len(names) - 50} more")

    # --- Page numbers & headings ---
    report_lines.append("\n## 2. Page Number & Heading Preservation")
    missing_page = []
    sample = []
    for e in entries[:10]:  # show first 10 as sample
        pg, line = detect_page_number(e)
        heads = detect_headings(e)
        status = "OK" if pg else "MISSING"
        if not pg:
            missing_page.append(e["name"])
        sample.append(f"- `{e['name']}`: page={pg}, headings={len(heads)}")
        if heads:
            sample[-1] += f" (e.g., `{heads[0][:80]}`)"
    report_lines.extend(sample)
    report_lines.append(f"\nFiles with no detectable page number in first 15 lines: {len(missing_page)}")
    if missing_page:
        report_lines.append("  - " + "\n  - ".join(missing_page[:20]))

    # --- Cleaning problems ---
    report_lines.append("\n## 3. Cleaning Problems Detected")

    # Hyphenation
    hypo_files = []
    for e in entries:
        hyps = find_hyphenation(e)
        if hyps:
            hypo_files.append((e["name"], len(hyps), hyps[0][:100]))
    report_lines.append(f"\n### Hyphenation across line breaks")
    report_lines.append(f"Files affected: {len(hypo_files)}")
    for name, count, ex in hypo_files[:10]:
        report_lines.append(f"- `{name}`: {count} instances, e.g. `{ex}`")

    # Table artifacts
    tbl_files = []
    for e in entries:
        tbls = find_table_artifacts(e)
        if tbls:
            tbl_files.append((e["name"], len(tbls), tbls[0][:100]))
    report_lines.append(f"\n### Possible table fragments (lines starting with \|)")
    report_lines.append(f"Files affected: {len(tbl_files)}")
    for name, count, ex in tbl_files[:10]:
        report_lines.append(f"- `{name}`: {count} lines, e.g. `{ex}`")

    # Repeated headers/footers
    repeated = find_repeated_lines(entries)
    report_lines.append(f"\n### Repeated lines across files (possible headers/footers)")
    report_lines.append(f"Lines appearing in >{int(REPEATED_LINE_THRESHOLD*100)}% of files: {len(repeated)}")
    for line, count, ratio in repeated[:20]:
        report_lines.append(f"- `{line}` — {count}/{len(entries)} files ({ratio})")

    # --- Summary ---
    report_lines.append("\n## 4. Summary")
    report_lines.append(f"- Total md files: {len(entries)}")
    report_lines.append(f"- Files missing page number: {len(missing_page)}")
    report_lines.append(f"- Files with hyphenation: {len(hypo_files)}")
    report_lines.append(f"- Files with table-like lines: {len(tbl_files)}")
    report_lines.append(f"- Repeated header/footer lines: {len(repeated)}")

    REPORT_PATH.write_text("\n".join(report_lines), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"\nInspection complete. Report written to {REPORT_PATH.resolve()}")
    print("\n".join(report_lines[-12:]))  # print summary to stdout


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## Reusable Cleaning Script

Save this as `scripts/clean_md.py`. It is designed to be run on your existing `./md/` files and on any new files produced by the extraction script, ensuring identical formatting.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
clean_md.py
Reusable cleaning script for vehicle service manual markdown extractions.

Rules:
- Removes running headers/footers and watermarks.
- Fixes hyphenation broken across line breaks.
- Normalizes whitespace while preserving tables and headings.
- Preserves page markers (e.g., "## Page 42").
- Repairs common markdown table pipe issues.
- Replaces em/en dashes used as empty-cell placeholders with "—".
"""

import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Patterns to strip (case-insensitive). Add to this list as you inspect more pages.
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    r"2014 F-150 Workshop Manual",
    r"Procedure revision date:\s*\d{2}/\d{2}/\d{4}",
    r"repair4less",
    r"SECTION\s+\d+-\d+:\s*[^\n]*",  # keep section title but strip if it repeats as header
]

# A page marker looks like: ## Page 42  or  # Page 42
PAGE_MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"^#{1,3}\s*Page\s+(\d+)\s*$", re.IGNORECASE)


def clean_text(text: str, force_page_marker: int | None = None) -> str:
    """Apply all cleaning rules to a single page's markdown text."""
    # 1. Remove headers/footers/watermarks
    for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS:
        text = re.sub(pat, "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)

    # 2. Fix hyphenation across line breaks: "brake-\nfluid" -> "brake fluid"
    text = re.sub(r"(\w)-\n(\w)", r"\1 \2", text)

    # 3. Repair tables: ensure every table row starts/ends with |
    lines = text.split("\n")
    fixed_lines = []
    in_table = False
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if stripped.startswith("|") and stripped.endswith("|"):
            in_table = True
            fixed_lines.append(stripped)
        elif in_table and stripped == "":
            # blank line inside table -> end of table
            in_table = False
            fixed_lines.append("")
        elif in_table and "|" in stripped:
            # row that lost border pipes; re-wrap
            fixed_lines.append(f"| {stripped} |")
        else:
            in_table = False
            fixed_lines.append(stripped)

    text = "\n".join(fixed_lines)

    # 4. Collapse 3+ blank lines to 2 (preserve paragraph breaks)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)

    # 5. Trim trailing whitespace on each line
    text = "\n".join(line.rstrip() for line in text.split("\n"))

    # 6. Ensure a page marker exists at top if requested
    if force_page_marker is not None:
        if not PAGE_MARKER_RE.search(text):
            text = f"---\n## Page {force_page_marker}\n---\n\n{text}"

    return text.strip()


def clean_file(path: Path, page_num: int | None = None, in_place: bool = True) -> str:
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    cleaned = clean_text(raw, force_page_marker=page_num)
    if in_place:
        path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
    return cleaned


def main():
    import argparse

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Clean service-manual markdown files.")
    parser.add_argument("input", nargs="?", default="./md", help="Directory of md files (default: ./md)")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="Output directory (default: in-place)")
    parser.add_argument("--start-page", type=int, default=1, help="Starting page number if markers missing")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    src = Path(args.input)
    dst = Path(args.out) if args.out else src
    dst.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    md_files = sorted(src.glob("*.md"))
    if not md_files:
        print(f"No markdown files found in {src}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

    for i, md_path in enumerate(md_files, start=args.start_page):
        out_path = dst / md_path.name
        page_num = i
        # Try to extract existing page number from content
        raw = md_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        m = PAGE_MARKER_RE.search(raw)
        if m:
            page_num = int(m.group(1))
        clean_file(md_path, page_num=page_num, in_place=(args.out is None))
        if args.out:
            out_path.write_text(clean_file(md_path, page_num=page_num, in_place=False), encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"Cleaned: {md_path.name} (page {page_num})")

    print(f"\nDone. {len(md_files)} file(s) processed.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

**Usage**
```bash
# Clean existing files in place
python scripts/clean_md.py ./md

# Or copy cleaned versions to ./md_cleaned/
python scripts/clean_md.py ./md --out ./md_cleaned
```

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
extract_remaining.py
Extract remaining PDF pages to markdown using PyMuPDF.
Resumable: skips pages that already have an md file in ./md/.
Uses the same cleaning rules as clean_md.py.

import re

        text = re.sub(r"(\w)-\n(\w)", r"\1 \2", text)
        text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)
        text = "\n".join(line.rstrip() for line in text.split("\n"))

    text = page.get_text("text", sort=True)
